# System Risk Register

Status: Active

Owner role: tracked queue for material security, data, PIT, schema, execution, and cross-LLM process risks that are not fixed immediately in the same reviewed slice.

This file exists to prevent audit findings from living only in chat. `docs/SESSION_LOG.md` records reasoning and review verdicts; this register records the durable open-risk queue. Do not use it as authorization to batch-fix everything at once. Each fix still follows `docs/AI_REVIEW_PROTOCOL.md`.

## Enforcement Rules

- Every material review or audit finding that affects data integrity, PIT safety, schema contract, execution simulation, security, ship-gate evidence, or cross-LLM continuity must be either fixed in the same reviewed slice or entered here before the round ends.
- `执行` must check this register before choosing the next task. Open P0 items outrank normal roadmap work unless the user explicitly approves a narrower override.
- `审查` must verify that new material findings were either fixed or registered here, and that resolved entries include concrete verification.
- `修复` may update this register only for approved Required fixes or Optional dispositions being handled in that repair round.
- A risk entry is not closed by intent. Closure requires file / test evidence and a reviewed commit or an explicit accepted-risk decision from the user.

## Status And Severity

Severity:
- P0: blocks the next unsafe execution path or can contaminate evidence / official outputs.
- P1: high risk before broader implementation, provider work, long-lane work, or production-like use.
- P2: medium risk; must be queued and fixed before the affected subsystem is promoted.
- P3: low risk or documentation hygiene.

Status:
- `open`: accepted for tracking; not fixed.
- `in_progress`: current reviewed change is addressing it.
- `blocked`: needs user decision or external dependency.
- `resolved`: fixed and verified.
- `superseded`: replaced by more specific open / resolved entries; the underlying risk is not fixed merely because the parent summary was split.
- `accepted_risk`: user explicitly accepted the residual risk.
- `needs_revalidation`: audit claim is plausible enough to track, but line-level repo validation is still required before implementation.

A-short semantic-risk provider probe README status-invariant wording drift (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `docs/README.md`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-1 / route-doc status invariant / cross-LLM continuity`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-SEMANTIC-PROBE-README-STATUS-INVARIANT-DRIFT`): the code-level repair for `R-ASHORT-SEMANTIC-PROBE-CNINFO-BAD-ROW-CLEAR-STATUS` is correct, and the active README row correctly says provider-quality defects map to `unknown`; however the same row still summarizes `validate_probe_summary_consistency` as `failure⇔unknown invariant`. That phrase describes the pre-repair invariant and is now wrong: the implementation intentionally uses `not ok ⇒ unknown` plus `clear/risk ⇒ ok + quality-clean`; cninfo may have `ok=True` and `status='unknown'` when provider quality is invalid (future / unparseable / non-dict / missing-field / mapping-mismatch). Because `docs/README.md` is the active durable routing table, this stale phrase can mislead a future agent into restoring the old `ok⇔not-unknown` invariant and reintroducing the just-fixed status bug. Required repair: replace `failure⇔unknown invariant` in the semantic-risk provider probe row with wording aligned to implementation, e.g. `status invariant [not ok⇒unknown; clear/risk⇒ok+quality-clean]`. No code/schema/provider fetch/production behavior is authorized by this finding. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12)**: the README semantic-risk provider probe row's `validate_probe_summary_consistency` description now reads `status invariant [not ok⇒unknown; clear/risk⇒ok+quality-clean]`. Repo-wide scan confirms remaining `failure⇔unknown` occurrences are only in append-only historical SESSION_LOG entries and in this/the prior finding's narrative (describing the defect), not in any active contract claim; the active register Slice-1 entry already carries the corrected wording. Docs-only. Resolved: Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12.

A-short semantic-risk provider probe reports provider-quality-invalid cninfo rows as `clear_light` (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_probe_summary.schema.json` if status enum is extended, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-1 / per-code status semantics / unknown-not-clear`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-SEMANTIC-PROBE-CNINFO-BAD-ROW-CLEAR-STATUS`): the P1 feasibility bypass is repaired, but `classify_cninfo_code` still sets `status="clear_light"` whenever `ok=True` and there is no PIT-valid announcement, even if the provider returned future-dated rows, unparseable disclosure dates, or non-dict malformed rows. Codex synthetic no-network probes after the latest repair show `future`, `bad_date`, and `bad_shape` samples now correctly produce `feasible=false` and reject forged `feasible=true`, but the affected per-code object still reports `status='clear_light'` with `dates_pit_ok=false` and one of `n_future_dated` / `n_unparseable_dates` / `n_bad_shape` > 0. That is not "successfully checked and clear"; it is an unknown / provider-quality-invalid state and contradicts the design/route wording that failure/unknown must not be disguised as clear. The same semantic hole can exist for PIT rows whose required fields or `secCode` mapping fail: feasibility blocks, but the per-code status can still read `clear_light`. Required repair: define and enforce status semantics so `clear_light` is allowed only for a genuinely successful clear result (empty successful window, or PIT-clean non-risk rows with required fields and mapping valid). Any code with future rows, unparseable dates, malformed rows, missing required fields, or code-mapping mismatch must be `unknown` or a formally added invalid-status enum. If using `unknown`, update `_check_provider_counts` so `ok=True` provider-quality failures may be `unknown`, while transport failures still must be `unknown`. Add adversarial tests for future-only, unparseable-only, non-dict-only, missing-field, and mapping-mismatch rows not being `clear_light`, plus a consistency test that a summary containing such an invalid row labelled `clear_light` raises. No real provider fetch or production behavior is authorized by this finding. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, reused `unknown`)**: `classify_cninfo_code` now computes `quality_ok = (no future/unparseable/non-dict defects AND n_pit>0 AND required_fields_ok AND code_mapping_ok)` and assigns `status` as: not ok → `unknown`; ok + empty window → `clear_light`; ok + not quality_ok → `unknown`; ok + quality_ok + risk-keyword → `risk_candidate`; else `clear_light`. So future/unparseable/non-dict/missing-field/mapping-mismatch codes now read `unknown`, never `clear`. `_check_provider_counts` relaxed: the strict `ok ⇔ not-unknown` invariant became `not ok ⇒ unknown` (transport failure) for both providers + a cninfo per-code status-quality invariant (clear_light/risk_candidate require ok + clean + valid fields/mapping; risk_candidate requires PIT rows) while sina keeps `ok ⇒ not unknown`. Adversarial tests added (future / unparseable / non-dict / missing-field / mapping-mismatch → status `unknown`; + consistency test that a defective row labelled `clear_light` raises). Status enum unchanged (reused `unknown`); 44 tests pass; schema meta + py_compile OK. Resolved: Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12.

A-short semantic-risk provider probe ignores unparseable / malformed cninfo returned rows in feasibility gate (status `resolved` Claude `修复` + Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `tests/test_a_short_semantic_risk_probe.py`, `schemas/a_short_semantic_risk_probe_summary.schema.json` if fields are added; PIT label `A-short semantic-risk Slice-1 / cninfo provider-shape and disclosure-date gate`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-SEMANTIC-PROBE-CNINFO-BAD-DATE-SHAPE-BYPASS`): the future-date PIT leak was repaired, but returned rows with bad shape can still bypass feasibility. `classify_cninfo_code` filters `announcements` to dict rows before counting `n_returned`, so non-dict provider rows vanish completely. Rows with unparseable `announcementTime` set per-code `dates_pit_ok=False`, but because they yield `n_announcements=0`, they are excluded from the aggregate `announced` set and do not affect `n_dates_pit_ok`; a sample with 4 clean PIT-announced codes plus one unparseable-date returned row still produced `feasible=True`, no blocking reasons, and that bad-date code status `clear_light`. A sample with 4 clean PIT-announced codes plus one non-dict announcement row also produced `feasible=True` after silently treating the malformed row as no return. This violates the Slice-1 goal of proving cninfo field/date shape before Slice 2. Required repair: count raw returned items before filtering, expose and gate invalid returned rows (`n_unparseable_date_codes` / `n_bad_shape_codes` or equivalent), and ensure non-dict rows or unparseable disclosure dates cannot be reported as clean and cannot coexist with `feasible=True`. Add adversarial tests for unparseable dates, non-dict announcement rows, and mixed clean+bad-shape samples; keep the future-date PIT tests passing. No real provider fetch or production behavior is authorized by this finding. **Working-tree repaired (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12)**: `classify_cninfo_code` now counts `n_returned` on the RAW provider list BEFORE any `isinstance` filter, and partitions every returned row exactly into `n_announcements`(PIT dict+canonical+≤as_of) + `n_future_dated` + `n_unparseable_dates`(dict, bad/missing date) + `n_bad_shape`(non-dict), with a consistency assert that the four sum to `n_returned`; non-dict rows are no longer silently dropped. Three provider quality gates (`n_future_dated_codes==0`, `n_unparseable_date_codes==0`, `n_bad_shape_codes==0`) are now feasibility AND consistency conditions, so a malformed/unparseable/future row can neither be reported clean (`dates_pit_ok` == "all returned rows clean") nor coexist with `feasible=true`. Adversarial tests added: unparseable-date row counted-not-dropped, non-dict row counted-not-dropped, partition-sums-to-n_returned, and the exact Codex repros (4 clean + 1 unparseable-date code → feasible=false + reason; 4 clean + 1 non-dict-row code → feasible=false + reason). 43 tests pass; schema + consistency updated. Resolved: Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12.

Resolved: Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12.

A-short V14.3 strict main-board helper accepts malformed accepted-prefix codes (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `engine/data/a_share_board_scope.py`, `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_share_board_scope.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / strict A-share main-board universe integrity before bootstrap`; Required ID `R-V143-PREBOOTSTRAP-MAINBOARD-MALFORMED-LEAK`): the new inclusion-based `is_a_share_main_board` correctly rejects normal B-share / ChiNext / STAR / BSE codes, but it only checks `symbol.startswith(...)`. Independent probe showed malformed accepted-prefix values such as `600ABC.SH`, `60000.SH`, `6000000.SH`, `00000A.SZ`, and `002.SZ` return `True`. This contradicts the helper comment / runner docstring claim that unknown or malformed codes are rejected, and leaves a non-canonical code path able to enter the regime breadth universe. Required repair: require canonical six-digit Tushare-style symbols before applying the exact prefix whitelist (e.g. `^\d{6}\.(SH|SZ)$` plus SSE `600/601/603/605`, SZSE `000/001/002/003`), and add adversarial tests for non-six-digit and non-numeric accepted-prefix symbols. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `is_a_share_main_board` now requires `len(symbol)==6` and all-ASCII-digit BEFORE the prefix whitelist, so `600ABC.SH`/`60000.SH`/`6000000.SH`/`00000A.SZ`/`002.SZ`/`600000.BJ` are rejected; canonical 6-digit accepted prefixes still pass. Tests extended with those malformed-under-accepted-prefix negatives. Verification 218/218 OK.

A-short V14.3 main-board filter route-doc stale helper pointer (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `docs/README.md`, `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `engine/data/a_share_board_scope.py`; PIT label `process-only route-doc / cross-LLM continuity for strict main-board scoping`; Required ID `R-V143-PREBOOTSTRAP-MAINBOARD-ROUTE-DOC-DRIFT`): the implementation now filters the V14.3 runner through the new strict `engine.data.a_share_board_scope.is_a_share_main_board`, but `docs/README.md` still says `run_regime_step` filters via the old exclusion-based `is_main_board_ts_code` and only mentions excluding ChiNext / STAR / BSE. That route-table row is the durable owner pointer future agents will follow, so it can reintroduce the old helper choice despite the code fix. Required repair: update the V14.3 runner row in `docs/README.md` to name `is_a_share_main_board`, state the exact canonical format and accepted prefixes, and explicitly mention B-share / malformed rejection, aligned with governance/schema/design/tests. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** both V14.3 README rows now name `is_a_share_main_board` and state the contract (accepts ONLY canonical 6-digit `.SH` 600/601/603/605 + `.SZ` 000/001/002/003; rejects B-shares 200*/900*, ChiNext/STAR/BSE, malformed/unknown). No remaining `is_main_board_ts_code` reference in V14.3 docs. Verification 218/218 OK.

A-short V14.3 pre-bootstrap main-board filter B-share leak (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `engine/data/a_share_board_scope.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_share_board_scope.py`, `presets/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance_20260611.json`, `schemas/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance.schema.json`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / main-board A-share universe integrity before bootstrap`; Required ID `R-V143-PREBOOTSTRAP-MAINBOARD-FILTER-BSHARE-LEAK`): the current repair correctly tries to scope V14.3 regime breadth to the user's A-share main-board universe, and the governance/design now declare the exact universe as SSE `600/601/603/605` plus SZSE `000/001/002/003`, excluding ChiNext/STAR/BSE. However the implemented `main_board_only()` calls the shared `is_main_board_ts_code`, whose current logic is exclusion-based and treats any code not starting with `300/301/688/689/920/8/4` and not ending `.BJ` as `main`. Independent probe showed `main_board_only(["600000.SH","000001.SZ","002001.SZ","003001.SZ","200001.SZ","900901.SH","300750.SZ","688981.SH","920083.BJ"])` keeps `200001.SZ` and `900901.SH`, which are outside the declared A-share main-board prefix set. This can either fail the first bootstrap again if such symbols lack usable `stk_limit`, or contaminate the comparison ledger's breadth metrics with non-target symbols. Required repair: enforce the exact governance universe in the runner or tighten the shared board helper if that is the intended project-wide contract; reject B-share prefixes such as `200*` / `900*`, unknown/malformed codes, and existing non-main boards; add adversarial tests for those negatives plus the positive `600/601/603/605/000/001/002/003` cases; keep docs/governance/schema aligned to one source. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added an INCLUSION-based `is_a_share_main_board` to the shared `engine/data/a_share_board_scope.py` (accepts ONLY `.SH` 600/601/603/605 + `.SZ` 000/001/002/003; rejects B-shares 200*/900*, ChiNext/STAR/BSE, and unknown/malformed) — added as a NEW function so the many existing exclusion-based `is_main_board_ts_code` callers (A-long) are untouched. `run_regime_step.main_board_only` now uses `is_a_share_main_board`. Tests: shared-helper inclusion/rejection (incl. 200001.SZ/900901.SH/garbage/None) + runner `main_board_only` drops B-shares + the `_init_pro` delegation test (Optional). The Optional register/external-memory-reference wording was also dropped (docs state the main-board rule directly). Verification: full V14.3 + board-scope + route-doc 218/218 OK.

A-short V14.3 regime runner pre-bootstrap probe fixes — Tushare init + main-board universe (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `presets/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance_20260611.json`, `schemas/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance.schema.json`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / real-data de-risk before bootstrap`): a throwaway pre-bootstrap real-Tushare fetch probe (over the real latest data date 20260611; wrote nothing) found two issues that would have broken the first authorized bootstrap of the just-committed ②b-2 runner (`707d5d8`). **(1) Tushare init silent-empty**: `_init_pro` used `ts.set_token` + `ts.pro_api`, which hits Tushare's silent-empty-DataFrame failure (trade_cal/daily returned 0 rows) — a bootstrap would have silently fetched nothing and could persist an empty/garbage ledger as "success". Fixed to use the repo's sanctioned `runners.a_short_iv_feed_probe.init_tushare_pro(token)` (pins base URL, no set_token). **(2) Universe not scoped → fail-closed trip**: the full-market fetch included a BSE stock (`920083.BJ`) lacking a usable stk_limit, so the as_of fail-closed gate raised (1/5511 uncovered). Per the user's standing directive (A-shares are operated main-board only), `run_regime_step` now filters daily/stk_limit to main board via the strict inclusion-based `engine.data.a_share_board_scope.is_a_share_main_board` (see the `R-V143-PREBOOTSTRAP-MAINBOARD-FILTER-BSHARE-LEAK` entry for the B-share-leak follow-up), and the frozen governance `universe` const + design §2 are updated from generic "A-share universe" to "MAIN-BOARD A-share universe (SSE 600/601/603/605 + SZSE 000/001/002/003; excludes ChiNext/STAR/BSE)" so spec=impl. Re-probe after fixes: main-board as_of = 3190 stocks, 0 uncovered, `compute_regime_daily_features(20260611)` succeeds (limit_up=65/down=53/net=12/streak=3/promo=0.159/failed=0.387/pct_ma20=13.8/csi300=-0.55%/csi1000_below=True), row passes daily schema + ledger semantics. Tests added: `main_board_only` filters ChiNext/STAR/BSE; a .BJ-without-stk_limit frame fails closed unfiltered but computes after main-board filtering. Verification: full V14.3 + route-doc 214/214 OK. Boundary unchanged (comparison-only, V14.2 frozen, egs_main untouched); the bootstrap RUN remains a separate user-authorized `执行` + TUSHARE_TOKEN.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-2 runner short-calendar append failure (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / persisted ledger append calendar coverage`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-RUNNER-SHORT-CALENDAR-BLOCKS-WEEKLY-APPEND`): normal post-bootstrap weekly execution fetches only a short recent calendar window (`45` days) and passes that same calendar into `save_ledger` / `validate_ledger`, but the persisted ledger already contains older bootstrap rows and the sanctioned ledger validator requires the supplied trade calendar to cover the existing coverage. Independent probe with an existing 252-row ledger plus a recent 45-day calendar failed with `ValueError: invalid ledger: ledger contains dates not on the trade calendar`, so the first real steady weekly append after bootstrap is likely blocked even when new data are valid. Required repair: for existing ledgers, validate/plan against a calendar covering persisted ledger coverage plus the new run date (or fetch calendar from existing coverage start/last date through `as_of`) and add a regression test with a persisted 252-row ledger plus short recent fetch window. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `main()` builds the trade calendar to SPAN the persisted ledger (`coverage.start`→`as_of` for an existing ledger; ~400d back for bootstrap) while fetching `daily`/`stk_limit` only for the recent window actually computed (new dates + MA20 lookback). Test `test_weekly_append_with_spanning_calendar_and_narrow_daily` (252-row ledger + spanning calendar + 30-day daily → appends). Verification 212/212 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-2 runner thin bootstrap evidence gap (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / bootstrap minimum evidence coverage`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-RUNNER-THIN-BOOTSTRAP-COUNTS-EVIDENCE`): absent-ledger runs currently bootstrap from whatever calendar is provided, and an independent probe with only 30 synthetic trading dates wrote a 30-row ledger and produced panel/evidence counts. This violates the 252-day percentile-ledger intent and can make a failed/partial first execution look like a valid comparison artifact. Required repair: when the ledger is absent, non-bootstrap mode should reject; bootstrap mode should require the policy minimum eligible trading-day coverage before writing/counting evidence, with tests for missing-ledger non-bootstrap rejection and insufficient bootstrap coverage rejection. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `run_regime_step` gained `bootstrap` — an absent/empty ledger with `bootstrap=False` raises, and a bootstrap yielding `< BACKFILL_MIN_TRADING_DAYS` (252) rows raises (no evidence clock from an insufficient window); `main()` also refuses a first run without `--bootstrap`. Tests: no-ledger+no-bootstrap raise, 6-day bootstrap raise, 252-day bootstrap persists. Verification 212/212 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-2 runner IV feed validation gap (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `runners/a_short_iv_feed_build.py`, `schemas/a_short_iv_feed.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / IV percentile input integrity`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-RUNNER-IV-FEED-UNVALIDATED`): `iv_series_to_map()` blindly consumes any `series` rows and silently overwrites duplicate dates; a wrong-schema feed with duplicate `20240102` values and a future `29991231` row was accepted as `{'20240102': 95.0, '29991231': 88.0}`. Because IV percentile can directly trigger V14.3 defense logic, an unvalidated or corrupt IV feed can flip comparison labels. Required repair: validate the loaded IV feed against the feed schema and summary-consistency guard (or equivalent canonical-date/duplicate/range checks) before mapping, and add adversarial tests for wrong schema, duplicate dates, non-canonical/future dates, and non-finite/out-of-range values. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `iv_series_to_map` now calls `validate_iv_feed` first — jsonschema against `a_short_iv_feed.schema.json` + the feed's own `validate_feed_summary_consistency` (reuse single-source: strictly-ascending/no-dup/no-future trade_date, iv_value>0, percentile 0-100) — so a wrong-schema / duplicate / future feed raises before mapping. Tests: duplicate-date and wrong-schema feed rejected. Verification 212/212 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-2 runner CLI as_of leniency (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / authorized fetch date guard`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-RUNNER-CLI-ASOF-LENIENT-FETCH`): `main()` relies on `datetime.strptime(as_of, "%Y%m%d")`, which accepts malformed short dates such as `2024011` as `20240101`; an independent monkeypatch probe showed `_fetch_trade_calendar` was called with malformed `end=2024011`. The earlier ledger repairs made canonical date validation explicit, but this runner can still reach the authorized fetch layer before applying that guard. Required repair: reject non-canonical `YYYYMMDD` `--as-of` before `_init_pro()` or any provider fetch, using the ledger canonical-date helper or identical logic, and add a test proving malformed dates fail before fetch initialization. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `main()` calls `is_canonical_date(as_of)` (single-source ledger helper) and `SystemExit`s on a malformed `--as-of` before `_init_pro()` / any fetch. Test `test_noncanonical_as_of_rejected_before_fetch` (`2024011` → SystemExit, no provider touched). Optional findings also addressed: `save_comparison_records` now validates each record + rejects duplicate as_of; route docs narrowed to "CLI default writes under the lane; all writers refuse production `result/a_short` paths". Verification 212/212 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-2 regime comparison runner (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `runners/a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison_runner.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime runner / I/O + persistence / authorized bootstrap 执行`): the I/O + execution layer, deliberately a STANDALONE runner (NOT an egs_main side-output — egs_main is production-frozen-adjacent and the ledger amortizes the 252-day cost, so a standalone runner stays isolated; **design deviation from §5's "EGS run side-output", evaluated & documented**). A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-1 empty/no-eligible bootstrap calendar accepted by orchestration (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding Codex deeper `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_pipeline.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime weekly orchestration / bootstrap calendar coverage`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-PIPELINE-EMPTY-BOOTSTRAP-CALENDAR-ACCEPTED`): `extend_ledger` is now the public pure orchestration step for both one-time bootstrap and weekly increment, but it currently inherits the low-level ledger rule that an empty ledger can be valid pre-bootstrap. Probe: `extend_ledger(build_ledger([]), "20240105", [], provider)` and `extend_ledger(build_ledger([]), "20240105", ["20240110","20240111"], provider)` both returned a valid 0-row ledger with `coverage.n=0` and made zero provider calls. That is safe for `validate_ledger` as an envelope/pre-bootstrap gate, but unsafe for the orchestration function because slice ②b-2's bootstrap runner is expected to call `extend_ledger(empty, ...)` and persist the returned ledger; a failed/empty calendar fetch or wrong calendar range could be written as a successful empty bootstrap artifact. Required repair: in `extend_ledger`, reject empty final ledgers when bootstrapping/extending from an empty existing ledger and no eligible trading days `<= as_of` were available (or otherwise make the caller explicitly acknowledge pre-bootstrap/no-data mode, not the default workflow). Add tests for an empty calendar and for a calendar whose dates are all after `as_of`. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `extend_ledger` now raises when bootstrapping from an empty existing ledger produces no rows (empty/all-future calendar), so a bad calendar fetch can't be persisted as a successful empty bootstrap; idempotent non-empty rerun unaffected. Tests: empty calendar and all-after-as_of calendar both raise. Verification 200/200 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-1 trade-calendar iterator consumption in orchestration (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_pipeline.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime weekly orchestration / calendar materialization`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-PIPELINE-CALENDAR-ITERATOR-CONSUMED`): `extend_ledger(existing_ledger, as_of, trade_calendar, feature_provider)` accepts `trade_calendar: Iterable[str]` but passes the same iterable to `validate_ledger_for_append`, `plan_append`, and final `validate_ledger` without materializing it once. Because each lower-level helper materializes its own iterable, a one-shot generator is consumed by the first validation call. Probe: `extend_ledger(build_ledger([]), cal[-1], (d for d in cal), provider)` returned an empty valid ledger and made zero provider calls instead of bootstrapping the calendar rows; `weekly_regime_step` with the same generator failed later only because `classify_raw_regime` saw empty history. This reintroduces the prior generator-calendar bug class at the new orchestration layer and can let slice ②b-2 persist an empty bootstrap ledger if it supplies a generator. Required repair: materialize `trade_calendar` once at the start of `extend_ledger` / `weekly_regime_step` and reuse that list for every downstream helper call. Add tests proving generator calendars bootstrap and weekly-step correctly. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** both `extend_ledger` and `weekly_regime_step` now `list(trade_calendar)` once at the top and reuse it for all helper calls. Test: a generator calendar bootstraps the full rows. Verification 200/200 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-1 weekly comparison rerun is not idempotent for current as_of (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-12; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_pipeline.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime weekly orchestration / rerun recovery`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-PIPELINE-WEEKLY-RERUN-DUPLICATE-CURRENT`): `weekly_regime_step` always appends the newly built current comparison record to `prior_comparison_records`. If the prior comparison history already contains the same `as_of` from a previous successful or partial same-date run, the audited evidence path rejects the duplicate and the whole step fails. Probe: a fresh ledger through `as_of` plus `prior_comparison_records=[build_comparison_record(... as_of=same day)]` raised `ValueError: duplicate as_of`. The next ②b-2 persistence/EGS wiring slice needs repeatable weekly execution and recovery semantics; this function is only ledger-idempotent, not full-step idempotent. Required repair: before appending current, define same-date comparison-record handling: if the prior current record matches the newly built/audited record, reuse/dedupe it; if it differs, raise an immutable-history conflict. Add tests for same-date identical rerun success and same-date divergent conflict. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-12, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `weekly_regime_step` now checks `prior_comparison_records` for the current `as_of` before appending: if present with identical IMMUTABLE classification fields (`_same_classification`: regimes/divergence/fired_rule/window/flags/basis/boundary — forward_returns excluded as they are re-derived) → replace the prior row (dedupe); if classification differs → raise immutable-history conflict; output `comparison_records` sorted by `as_of`. Tests: identical same-week rerun → total_weeks stays 1; divergent same-week → raise; output sorted. Verification 200/200 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②b-1 regime weekly orchestration (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-12; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_pipeline.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime weekly orchestration / cadence workflow`): PURE orchestration composing the reviewed pieces with the data side INJECTED. `extend_ledger(existing, as_of, calendar, feature_provider)` encodes the fail-closed cadence workflow (`validate_ledger_for_append` → `plan_append` → `feature_provider(d)` per planned day [must be dated exactly d] → `merge_rows` → `build_ledger` → `validate_ledger`); the same function does bootstrap (empty→last 252) and weekly increment, is idempotent on rerun, materializes the calendar once (generator-safe), rejects a gappy existing ledger, and rejects an empty/all-future bootstrap calendar. `weekly_regime_step` composes extend + `build_comparison_record` + audited backfill/summarize/render (PIT-capped at the run date), with same-week rerun semantics (identical classification → dedupe-replace; divergent → immutable-history conflict raise) and `as_of`-sorted output → {ledger, comparison_record, comparison_records, evidence, panel_markdown}. 13 tests (bootstrap, increment, idempotent, provider-wrong-date/gappy/empty-cal/all-future raise, generator calendar, step-from-empty, step-accumulates-prior, identical-rerun dedupe, divergent-rerun raise, sorted output). **No Tushare fetch, no EGS wiring, no file write, no production** — those are slice 2b-impl ②b-2 (real provider + persistence + in-EGS side-output + bootstrap-runner CLI [bootstrap RUN = user 执行 + TUSHARE_TOKEN] + panel-into-weekly-report). Pre-flight self-sweep run before review. Verification: `tests.test_a_short_regime_pipeline` 13/13 + full V14.3 200/200 + route-doc OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②a future-as_of record can advance evidence counts (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-11; finding Codex deeper `审查` FAIL 2026-06-11; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_comparison.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison.py`; PIT label `comparison-only forward-live evidence clock / as_of cap`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-FUTURE-ASOF-COUNTS`): the audited evidence path requires a canonical `as_of_now`, but it never rejects comparison records whose own `as_of` is later than that cap. Such a future record is treated like an anchor-missing row: all forward returns remain pending, but the row still advances `total_weeks` and `divergence_weeks`, and a future current record can be rendered in the panel. Independent probe with `as_of_now=20240112` and record `as_of=20991231` returned `{'total_weeks': 1, 'divergence_weeks': 1, 'backfill_complete_weeks': 0}` from `summarize_comparison_records`, and `render_regime_comparison_block` rendered the future `as_of`. Required repair: in the sanctioned summarize/render path, reject any current/history comparison record with `as_of > as_of_now` before counting or display; add tests for future history rejection and future current-record render rejection. `backfill_forward_returns` may stay a lower-level helper only if the evidence/render path remains stricter. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added `_audited_history` (backfill-audit + reject `as_of > as_of_now` future records + reject duplicate `as_of`), used by `summarize_comparison_records`; `render_regime_comparison_block` rejects a current record dated after `as_of_now` and audits history via `_audited_history`. Tests: future history record → summarize raises; future current → render raises. Verification 187/187 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②a render history can exclude or contradict the displayed current record (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-11; finding Codex deeper `审查` FAIL 2026-06-11; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_comparison.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison.py`; PIT label `comparison-only panel evidence progress / current-history consistency`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-RENDER-HISTORY-CURRENT-MISMATCH`): `render_regime_comparison_block(record, csi1000, as_of_now, records=...)` audits and displays the current `record`, but evidence progress is summarized from arbitrary `records` without requiring that the displayed current week is present and payload-consistent in that history. Probe: current `record.as_of=20240107` with V14.3 `attack` divergence rendered as the current panel row, while `records=[20240102 shock/non-divergent]` produced evidence progress `1/12` and divergence `0`; the displayed divergent week was excluded from the counter. Add tests for missing current in `records` and mismatched same-`as_of` payload. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `render_regime_comparison_block` now requires (when `records` is given) that the audited history contain exactly one row matching the audited current record's `as_of`, and that that row equals the audited current record — else raises (missing / payload-mismatch); the displayed evidence counts are derived from that same audited history. Tests: current absent from `records` → raise; same-`as_of` different payload → raise. Verification 187/187 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②a audited evidence path `as_of_now` optional look-ahead (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-11; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-11; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_comparison.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison.py`; PIT label `comparison-only forward-return evidence clock / no-look-ahead cap`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-AUDITED-PATH-ASOF-NOW-OPTIONAL`): `summarize_comparison_records(records, csi1000, as_of_now=None)` and `render_regime_comparison_block(record, csi1000, as_of_now=None, ...)` document `as_of_now` as REQUIRED, but the signature defaults it to `None` and tests exercise the default. Independent probe with an 11-day CSI1000 series and a 20240102 record: `summarize_comparison_records([record], idx)` returned `backfill_complete_weeks=1`, and `render_regime_comparison_block(record, idx)` rendered fully filled forward returns, even though no as-of cap was supplied. This is a look-ahead path for the evidence clock whenever the caller passes an index frame containing future rows. Required repair: for the sanctioned evidence/render path, make `as_of_now` truly mandatory and canonical. `summarize_comparison_records` and `render_regime_comparison_block` should raise when `as_of_now is None`; add tests proving omitted `as_of_now` raises, and proving an as-of cap at the anchor leaves all forward horizons pending even if the supplied CSI1000 frame contains future rows. `backfill_forward_returns` may remain a lower-level pure helper with optional `as_of_now` only if docs/tests clearly distinguish it from the sanctioned evidence path. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `summarize_comparison_records` and `render_regime_comparison_block` now take `as_of_now` with NO default and raise `ValueError` if it is None or non-canonical (omitting → TypeError), so the sanctioned evidence/render path is always PIT-capped + audited. `backfill_forward_returns` stays the lower-level helper (optional `as_of_now`), documented as such. Tests: omitted/None/non-canonical `as_of_now` raise; an as-of cap at the anchor leaves all forward horizons pending (backfill_complete_weeks=0) even with a future-containing CSI1000 frame. Verification 183/183 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②a existing forward-return PIT/conflict bypass (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-11; finding Codex deeper re-`审查` FAIL 2026-06-11; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_comparison.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison.py`; PIT label `comparison-only forward-return backfill / evidence-clock existing-value audit`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-EXISTING-FWD-RETURN-PIT-CONFLICT-BYPASS`): `backfill_forward_returns` preserves every existing non-null horizon (`continue`) and then only validates shape/finite-ness, so prefilled values can bypass both the no-look-ahead check and the const-pinned CSI1000 return-basis check. Independent probes: (1) record with existing `h1=999.0` while CSI1000 close 100→101 stayed `h1=999.0`, passed `validate_comparison_record`, counted as `backfill_complete_weeks=1`, and rendered to the panel; (2) with `as_of_now='20240102'` (anchor day; no forward horizon elapsed), a record prefilled with all h1/h3/h5/h10 values stayed `backfill_complete=True` and counted as evidence. Required repair: keep no-overwrite only for matching audited values. For every existing non-null horizon, if the target trading day is not available under `as_of_now`, raise; if target is available, recompute the const-pinned CSI1000 return and raise on mismatch beyond a tight deterministic tolerance. Add tests for mismatched existing h1, existing not-yet-elapsed horizon, and matching existing h1 preserved. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `backfill_forward_returns` no longer blindly `continue`s existing non-null horizons. For each existing non-null horizon: if the target is not available under `as_of_now` (not elapsed / anchor or target out of the finite-positive series) → RAISE; if available → recompute the const-pinned CSI1000 return and RAISE if `abs(existing − recomputed) > 1e-6`; only an audited matching value is preserved. Tests: existing `h1=999.0` mismatch → raise; prefilled `h1` with `as_of_now`=anchor (not elapsed) → raise; matching `h1=1.0` preserved without change. README ②a row, cadence design §5 ②a, and the active register summary updated from "fills only nulls/never overwrites" to the audited-existing-value contract. Verification 179/179 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ②a comparison evidence integrity gaps (status `resolved` committed local master 2026-06-11; finding Codex `审查` FAIL 2026-06-11; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_comparison.py`, `engine/a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `schemas/a_short_regime_comparison_weekly.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_comparison.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_classifier.py`; PIT label `comparison-only forward-return backfill / switch-candidate evidence clock`; Required IDs `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-UNVALIDATED-EVIDENCE-COUNT`, `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-ASOF-CANONICAL-GAP`, `R-V143-SLICE2B-COMPARISON-NONPOSITIVE-INDEX-CLOSE`): The slice is not commit-safe. (1) `summarize_comparison_records` counts history records without the sanctioned `validate_comparison_record`, does not reject duplicate `as_of`, and `render_regime_comparison_block` displays those counts as evidence progress; probes showed an invalid record counted as complete evidence and duplicate weeks displayed as `3/12`. A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① non-finite numeric input gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / finite numeric input contract`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-NONFINITE-NUMERIC-INPUTS`): The implementation documents usable prices/limits as finite positive, but `_usable_price_codes` and `_usable_limit_codes` only check `notna` and `>0`, so `+Inf` passes as usable. Independent probes showed as_of `daily.close=high=inf` emits `limit_up_count=1`, as_of `stk_limit.up_limit=down_limit=inf` emits `limit_down_count=1`, and as_of `csi300.close=inf` returns `csi300_ret_1d=inf` (then `daily_row_semantic_errors` rejects it, proving the producer can return semantic-invalid rows). Required repair: enforce finite numeric values after coercion for daily `close/high`, `stk_limit` `up_limit/down_limit`, and index `close`; invalid as_of daily/limit data remains fail-closed, while invalid/stale index data should become unavailable/null+flag instead of non-finite output. Add adversarial tests for `+Inf` / `-Inf` across daily price, limit price, and index close; preferably self-validate the row before returning. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** switched the usable checks from `notna & >0` to `np.isfinite(...) & >0` for daily `close/high`, `stk_limit` `up/down`, and index `close` (isfinite excludes NaN AND ±Inf). Invalid as_of daily/limit → fail closed; ±Inf index close → filtered → last row ≠ as_of → null + `csi*_unavailable`. Added a producer self-check: the returned row is run through `daily_row_semantic_errors` and a non-empty result raises (a semantic-invalid row can no longer escape). Tests: `+Inf` as_of price → raise, `+Inf` as_of limit → raise, `+Inf` index close → unavailable. Verification 161/161 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① history-incomplete operands still consumed (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `engine/a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_classifier.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer/classifier / history-dependent operands`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-HISTORY-INCOMPLETE-OPERANDS-CONSUMED`): Prior-window bad data is flagged with `stk_limit_history_incomplete`, but history-dependent operands can still be emitted and consumed. Probe: a 20-day window with one invalid prior close emitted `pct_above_ma20=100.0` plus `stk_limit_history_incomplete`, not null. A classifier probe with five rows carrying `pct_above_ma20=0.0`, `csi1000_below_ma20=True`, and `data_quality_flags=['stk_limit_history_incomplete']` still classified `contraction/slow_bleed`; the flag is surfaced but does not mask the operand. Required repair: either null/mask all affected history-dependent operands when the needed prior window is incomplete (`pct_above_ma20`, `max_limit_streak`, and any contraction/attack operands derived from them), or update classifier logic to refuse `slow_bleed` / `streak_collapse` / affected attack rules when the relevant history-incomplete flag is present. Add producer and classifier tests proving history-incomplete rows cannot fire or suppress these rules as real observations. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** chose the classifier-refusal option (the non-nullable `max_limit_streak` can't be nulled, so masking is the uniform fix). `classify_raw_regime` now reads the as_of row's `data_quality_flags`; when `stk_limit_history_incomplete` is present it masks ALL history-dependent rules — contraction (streak_collapse/earning_effect_gone/slow_bleed) set to `[]` and attack blocked — leaving only today-only defense (iv/limit_down/index-crash/exhaustion-with-already-nulled-promotion) and shock reachable. Added `history_masked` to the classifier output for transparency. Classifier tests: slow_bleed, streak_collapse, and attack each suppressed under the flag → shock; defense (broad-index crash) still fires. Verification 161/161 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① as_of `daily` price-quality gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex two-round re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / current-day price usability`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-ASOF-DAILY-PRICE-QUALITY`): The fail-closed repair covers bad as_of `stk_limit`, but current-day `daily` price fields are still silently coerced. Probe with as_of `close/high = NaN` emitted a schema-valid row with zero limit counts and no price-quality flag; probe with as_of `close/high = 0` emitted `limit_down_count=1`, `net_limit=-1`, schema OK, and no ledger semantic error. These are broken price rows, not real breadth observations. Required repair: before limit breadth is computed, require every as_of traded stock to have usable finite positive `close` and `high`, and reject impossible `high < close` at minimum. Bad as_of price data should fail closed; prior-window daily-price gaps used by MA/streak/promotion need explicit null/flag behavior, not silent coercion. Add adversarial tests for NaN/nonnumeric/zero/negative `close/high` and `high < close`. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added `_usable_price_codes` (finite positive close & high, high>=close); the as_of fail-closed gate now requires BOTH usable price AND usable limit per traded stock (`_usable_codes = _usable_price_codes ∩ _usable_limit_codes`), so bad as_of price raises; prior-window price gaps fold into `_incomplete` → `stk_limit_history_incomplete` flag + promotion null (no silent coercion). Test `test_bad_asof_daily_price_fails_closed` (NaN, zero, high<close). Verification 154/154 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① source-panel duplicate-row gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex two-round re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / source panel uniqueness`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-SOURCE-PANEL-DUPLICATE-ROWS`): The producer does not enforce uniqueness in its source panels. Duplicate conflicting `daily` rows for one `(trade_date, ts_code)` can fabricate `limit_up_count=1` or `failed_limit_rate=1.0`; duplicate conflicting `stk_limit` rows can fabricate `limit_up_count=1` by matching a bogus lower `up_limit`; duplicate current-day index rows produced `csi300_ret_1d=-50.0` from two same-date rows and would be consumed as a broad-index-crash signal. All probes pass the ledger semantic validator because output cross-field invariants still look valid. Required repair: enforce one row per `(trade_date, ts_code)` in `daily` and `stk_limit`, and one row per `trade_date` in each index panel for all rows used at or before `as_of`; reject duplicates, or only allow exact identical duplicates after a documented deterministic de-dup. Add adversarial tests for conflicting duplicate daily, limit, and index rows. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added `_assert_unique(df, keys, name, as_of)` raising on any duplicate key among rows at/<= as_of; applied to `daily`/`stk_limit` (`trade_date, ts_code`) and `csi300`/`csi1000` (`trade_date`) before any metric. Rejects all duplicates (chose reject over dedup — safer for the evidence clock). Tests: conflicting duplicate daily, stk_limit, and index rows all raise. Verification 154/154 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① source trade-date canonical gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex two-round re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / lexicographic PIT precondition`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-SOURCE-TRADE-DATE-CANONICAL-GAP`): The producer validates `as_of`, but `daily` / `stk_limit` / index `trade_date` values are cast to strings and compared/sorted lexicographically without source-date validation. A malformed prior `daily.trade_date="2026-01-01"` was accepted and is invisible to output semantic validation because the output row's `as_of` is valid. This reopens the date-order/PIT class already closed in the ledger slice: lexicographic filters are only sound after every used source date is real canonical `YYYYMMDD`. Required repair: validate source `trade_date` values in `daily`, `stk_limit`, `csi300`, and `csi1000` for the producer's used universe before sorting/filtering; reject noncanonical or impossible dates. Add tests for malformed daily/limit/index dates. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added `_assert_canonical_panel_dates` (reuses the single-source `is_canonical_date`) applied to `daily`/`stk_limit`/`csi300`/`csi1000` before any sort/filter, raising on any non-canonical `trade_date`. Test `test_noncanonical_panel_date_rejected` (`2026-01-01` prior daily date raises). Verification 154/154 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① fail-closed contract documentation drift (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex two-round re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`, `docs/README.md`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / future wiring contract`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-DOC-CONTRACT-DRIFT-AFTER-FAIL-CLOSED`): The code contract changed to fail-closed for as_of limit coverage, but durable text still says missing-day `stk_limit` degrades with a flag / honest degradation. Examples include the producer inputs docstring, test module header, docs route row 70, and the cadence design §5 slice-2b-impl description. Future slice ② wiring could follow the stale docs and reintroduce the old fail-open behavior. Required repair: update those docs/test descriptions to the actual layered contract: as_of non-nullable count inputs fail closed; nullable or prior-window metrics degrade only where explicitly tested. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** rewrote the producer main docstring (layered contract: source-panel integrity raises / as_of fail-closed on price+limit / nullable+prior-window degrade) + the module inputs docstring (no more "missing→flag"), the test module header, the README route row, and cadence design §5 ① — all now state fail-closed for as_of non-nullable count inputs and flag-degrade only for nullable/prior-window. Verification 154/154 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① as_of `stk_limit_missing` still fabricates limit-derived metrics (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `engine/a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_classifier.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / classifier consumption of missing limit data`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-ASOF-LIMIT-MISSING-METRIC-FABRICATION`): The repair flags fully missing as-of `stk_limit` with `stk_limit_missing`, but still emits limit-derived integer metrics as real zeros and can emit `promotion_rate=0.0` when the prior-day denominator is valid. Probe: prior day has 5 limit-up stocks, current day has daily rows but no current `stk_limit`; output is `limit_up_count=0`, `max_limit_streak=0`, `promotion_rate=0.0`, `stk_limit_missing`. A direct classifier probe then classified a row with `stk_limit_missing` and `max_limit_streak=0` as `contraction/streak_collapse` because `classify_raw_regime` ignores the limit-data flags. This means missing current-day limit data can create a false V14.3 contraction label. Required repair: missing/partial/unusable as-of limit data must not feed limit-dependent raw rules as real observations. Acceptable fixes include fail-closing producer output for as-of limit-data absence, or updating the producer/classifier contract so `stk_limit_missing` / `stk_limit_partial` / unusable-limit flags null or mask all limit-derived operands (`limit_down_count`, `net_limit`, `max_limit_streak`, `promotion_rate`, `failed_limit_rate`) before classification. Add tests proving missing as-of limit data cannot produce `promotion_rate=0.0` and cannot fire `streak_collapse`, exhaustion, defense limit-down, or attack rules. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** chose **fail-closed** (the limit count fields are non-null integers that cannot honestly encode "unknown", so a fabricated 0 is itself the defect). `compute_regime_daily_features` now RAISES if any as_of traded stock lacks a usable limit (so no fabricated-zero row is ever produced → nothing reaches `classify_raw_regime`; the classifier needs no change). Prior-day window incompleteness degrades instead: `promotion_rate=None` + `stk_limit_history_incomplete` flag (never `0.0` from missing data). Tests: `test_missing_asof_limit_fails_closed` (raises), `test_partial_asof_limit_fails_closed` (raises), `test_partial_stk_limit_prior_day_nulls_promotion` (promotion null + history flag, not 0.0). The earlier partial→flag-and-keep-counts behavior was intentionally escalated to fail-closed to satisfy this finding. Verification 149/149 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① unusable `stk_limit` values are treated as valid coverage (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / usable limit-price coverage`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-UNUSABLE-LIMIT-VALUES-SILENT`): `_uncovered(day)` only checks whether a `stk_limit` row exists for each `ts_code`, while `_limit_sets` marks `have_limit=True` as soon as the merge is non-empty. If rows exist but `up_limit/down_limit` are NaN, nonnumeric, or non-positive, the producer emits `limit_up_count=0`, `limit_down_count=0`, no `stk_limit_missing` / `stk_limit_partial` / limit-quality flag, schema OK, and no ledger semantic error. Probe with two current daily stocks and two unusable limit rows (`up_limit=NaN` / `up_limit=0`) reproduced exactly that. Required repair: define "covered" as having usable finite positive `up_limit` and `down_limit` values for the stock/date, not merely row presence; unusable values must be treated as missing/partial or fail closed. Add adversarial tests for NaN, nonnumeric, zero, and negative limit values. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added `_usable_limit_codes(limit_day)` = ts_codes with finite positive up_limit AND down_limit (NaN/nonnumeric→`_num`→NaN→excluded; zero/negative→excluded). Coverage (`_incomplete`) now requires usable values, not row presence; combined with the as_of fail-closed gate, an as_of with any unusable limit raises. Test `test_unusable_asof_limit_values_fail_closed` (NaN up, zero up, zero down, negative down all raise). Verification 149/149 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① partial `stk_limit` coverage silently treated as complete (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex `审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / limit breadth integrity`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-PARTIAL-STK-LIMIT-SILENT`): `_limit_sets` inner-joins `daily_day` to `stk_limit` and returns `have_limit=True` as soon as any matched stock exists. `compute_regime_daily_features` only flags `stk_limit_missing` when the entire day has no usable limit rows. Therefore a partial `stk_limit` panel (for example 2 daily stocks but only 1 limit row) emits `limit_up_count=1` with no limit-data quality flag, passes schema, and has no ledger semantic error. This can undercount/overstate limit-up/down breadth, failed-limit rate, max streak, and promotion-rate denominator, then contaminate the 252d comparison ledger. Required repair: detect partial `stk_limit` coverage per day (at minimum as_of and previous day used for promotion, preferably all dates used for streak), either fail closed or emit explicit null/degraded metrics plus a `stk_limit_partial`/coverage flag; do not compute full-universe breadth from a subset silently. Add adversarial tests for partial as_of coverage and partial prior-day promotion coverage. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added `_uncovered(day)` (daily-traded stocks lacking a stk_limit row); `compute_regime_daily_features` now flags `stk_limit_partial` when ANY window day used by counts/streak/promotion is incomplete (as_of has some coverage; a fully-missing as_of stays `stk_limit_missing`), and **nulls the coverage-sensitive ratios** (`failed_limit_rate` on partial as_of; `promotion_rate` on partial as_of OR partial prior day). Integer counts stay (schema requires non-null) but are flagged as lower bounds. Tests: partial-as_of (flag + failed_limit_rate null) and partial-prior-day (promotion null + flag). Verification 148/148 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① stale index row reused when index lacks `as_of` (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex `审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / index date alignment`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-STALE-INDEX-ASOF`): `_index_ret_and_below` filters index rows to `<= as_of` and computes return / MA from the last available row without requiring that row's `trade_date == as_of`. A missing current-day CSI300/CSI1000 row is therefore treated as valid by silently reusing a prior day; probe showed `as_of=20240105` with index rows only through `20240104` returned finite CSI returns and did not flag `csi300_unavailable`. This can suppress or fabricate broad-index-crash and slow-bleed operands. Required repair: require latest index row exactly equals `as_of` before emitting `ret_1d` or `csi1000_below_ma20`; otherwise return null and flag unavailable/stale. Add adversarial tests for stale CSI300 and stale CSI1000 panels. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `_index_ret_and_below` now returns `(None, None)` unless the latest index row at/<= as_of has `trade_date == as_of`, so a stale/missing current-day index → null + the caller's `csi300_unavailable` / `csi1000_unavailable` flag (no stale ret or stale below_ma20 emitted). Test `test_stale_index_flagged_unavailable` (index through as_of−1 → both indices unavailable). Verification 148/148 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① producer can emit schema-invalid rows for invalid date / IV inputs (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex `审查` FAIL; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / output contract`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-FEATURES-SCHEMA-INVALID-OUTPUT-GUARDS`): The producer documentation says the returned row is intended to pass `a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json` and ledger `daily_row_semantic_errors`, but the function does not validate canonical real `YYYYMMDD` before using lexicographic PIT filters and accepts any finite IV value. Probes showed `as_of="2024011"` with matching daily rows returns a schema-invalid row, and `iv_percentile_252d=150.0` returns `150.0` with no `iv_unavailable` flag even though the daily schema max is 100. Downstream ledger validation would catch some writes, but the producer itself currently violates its own contract and leaves a footgun for slice 2b-impl ② wiring. Required repair: reject non-canonical real `YYYYMMDD` `as_of` before filtering, and treat IV outside `[0,100]` as invalid (null+flag or fail closed, but not schema-invalid output). Add adversarial tests for malformed `as_of`, impossible dates, negative IV, and IV >100. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `compute_regime_daily_features` now raises on a non-canonical `as_of` (reuses the now-public single-source `engine.a_short_regime_ledger.is_canonical_date` — `_is_canonical_date` kept as internal alias), before any lexicographic filter; IV outside `[0,100]` (also NaN/Inf/None) → `None` + `iv_unavailable`. Tests: malformed/impossible `as_of` (`2024011`/`20240231`/`202401 1`) raise; IV `150`/`-5` → null+flag. Verification 148/148 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-impl ① regime daily-feature computation (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_features.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_features.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature producer / limit caliber / PIT`): PURE producer `compute_regime_daily_features` computing one `a_short_market_regime_daily` row from in-memory panels, faithful to slice-1 governance calibers (per-stock stk_limit up/down → ±10/20/ST±5; failed_limit_rate = touched-up-closed-below / touched; max consecutive limit-up streak; promotion_rate prior-day-up→today-up / denom, denom<5→null+insufficient_sample; pct_above_ma20 20d-window-or-null; csi300/csi1000 ret_1d + csi1000_below_ma20; iv passthrough from IV feed with NaN/None→null+iv_unavailable). PIT (rows>as_of ignored), honest degradation flags, never fabricates; output passes the daily schema + ledger `daily_row_semantic_errors`. 19 tests incl. ST-caliber, zero/thin denom, short window, missing index/iv, PIT. **No data fetch, no EGS wiring, no file write, no production** — those are slice 2b-impl ② (in-EGS fetch+append + bootstrap runner [needs user 执行 + TUSHARE_TOKEN] + panel + forward backfill). A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence plan_append stale pre-gate docstring (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex final re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / API documentation / safe append wiring`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-PLANAPPEND-DOCSTRING-STALE-PREGATE`): After the gate split, the correct pre-planning gate is `validate_ledger_for_append`, while the final/read gate is `validate_ledger`. The `plan_append` docstring still says the safe pre-planning reason is that sanctioned `validate_ledger` rejects gappy ledgers and that callers must validate before planning. That wording recreates the earlier append pre-gate contradiction for future implementers: `validate_ledger` enforces freshness and rejects the normal prior-run ledger before append. Required repair: update the `plan_append` docstring to name `validate_ledger_for_append` as the required pre-append historical gate and reserve `validate_ledger` for the merged final/read gate; add a docstring assertion only if local testing conventions make that worthwhile. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** rewrote the `plan_append` Note to require `validate_ledger_for_append` as the pre-append gate and to explicitly warn against using `validate_ledger` there (it adds freshness and would reject the normal prior-run ledger; reserved for the merged final/read gate). Docstring-only; behavior + tests unchanged (125/125 OK). No code-pattern for docstring assertions in this repo, so text fix per Codex's allowance.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence API hardening / schema-doc drift (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex two-round re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `schemas/a_short_regime_daily_ledger.schema.json`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_ledger_cadence_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / implementation-proofing / schema-vs-gate clarity`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-API-HARDENING-DOC-DRIFT`): Fresh second-pass review found lower-severity but real footguns around the still-open P1 blockers. `validate_ledger()` consumes `trade_calendar` twice, so a valid generator calendar is falsely rejected as "dates not on calendar"; `plan_append(..., backfill_min=0)` returns the full eligible calendar and `backfill_min=-1` returns a sliced subset instead of rejecting invalid policy input; `plan_append(["20240101","20240101"], ...)` silently deduplicates corrupt existing dates; `merge_rows()` accepts semantic-invalid new rows such as inconsistent `net_limit` unless final validation is called; the design doc still says "ledger 已到/超 as_of -> 返回 []" while repaired code raises on `max(existing)>as_of`; and the ledger schema text overclaims that `merge_rows` enforces daily-row validity even though generic schema-only validation accepts a row with only `as_of`. These should be repaired or explicitly narrowed while fixing the P1s: materialize `trade_calendar` once, reject invalid `backfill_min`, reject duplicate `existing_dates` or require/route through the new append pre-gate, clarify `merge_rows` is merge-only and final/current validation is mandatory before write/read, and align design/schema wording with actual gate responsibilities. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** (1) `validate_ledger` now materializes `trade_calendar` once (`cal_entries` reused for both the canonical check and the `cal` set), so a generator calendar is no longer falsely rejected; (2) `plan_append` rejects `backfill_min <= 0`; (3) `plan_append` rejects duplicate `existing_dates` (no silent dedup); (4) `merge_rows` docstring clarified merge-only + gates mandatory before write/read; (5) design doc corrected: `==as_of`→`[]` (idempotent) vs `>as_of`→raise; (6) ledger schema description corrected to state `merge_rows` enforces only append-only immutability, daily-row validity is the gates'. Tests: generator calendar accepted (validate + plan_append), `backfill_min` 0/-1 rejected, duplicate existing rejected. Verification 125/125 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2b-cadence canonical-date leniency gap (status `resolved` Codex PASS + committed local master 2026-06-11; finding 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` FAIL; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_ledger.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_ledger.py`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily-feature ledger / canonical YYYYMMDD / string-order PIT precondition`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2B-CANONICAL-DATE-LENIENCY-GAP`): The repaired `_is_canonical_date()` relies only on `datetime.strptime(s, "%Y%m%d")`, which is lenient: Python accepts non-canonical strings such as `2024011` and `202401 1` as 2024-01-01. This reopens the date-semantics/PIT ordering risk because lexicographic comparisons are only sound after exactly-8-digit canonical dates are guaranteed. Required repair: require `isinstance(str)`, exactly 8 ASCII digits, successful parse, and `strftime("%Y%m%d") == s`; add adversarial tests for 7-digit and whitespace-containing `as_of` and calendar entries through both `validate_ledger` and `plan_append`. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** `_is_canonical_date` now requires `isinstance(str)` + exactly 8 ASCII digits (`all(c in "0123456789")`) + `strptime(s).strftime("%Y%m%d") == s` round-trip, so `2024011` / `202401 1` / leading-or-trailing-space strings are rejected. Added 5 adversarial tests (existing-future merge, future-contaminated plan_append, validate-with-as_of future row, mandatory-as_of/calendar TypeError, envelope-accepts-but-full-gate-rejects gap). A-short V14.3 slice 2a producer schema-validation gap (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master; severity `P1`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `schemas/a_short_regime_comparison_weekly.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_classifier.py`; PIT label `comparison-record producer / evidence-clock artifact validity`; Required ID `R-V143-SLICE2A-BUILDER-SCHEMA-INVALID-OUTPUT`): The repair correctly added `validate_comparison_record()` for cross-field invariants and const-pinned `forward_return_basis`, but `build_comparison_record()` only calls that bespoke validator before returning; it does not validate field-local schema constraints or forward-return value types. Required repair: make the sanctioned producer reject schema-invalid forward return payloads before returning (either by calling schema validation in the builder/writer path or by adding equivalent exact horizon-key + number/null type checks to `validate_comparison_record`), and add adversarial tests proving non-number return values and missing/extra horizon keys are rejected at the producer/validator layer.
