Stale `forward excess` docstring corrected to CSI1000 raw forward return. Adversarial tests added: builder rejects non-number `h1`; validator rejects non-number value, missing horizon key, and extra horizon key. Verification: `tests.test_a_short_regime_classifier` 44/44 (+4) + governance + route-doc = 72/72 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2a comparison-record evidence-contract gaps (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master; severity `P1`; scope `schemas/a_short_regime_comparison_weekly.schema.json`, `engine/a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime evidence clock / forward-return backfill / switch-candidate integrity`; Required IDs `R-V143-SLICE2A-COMPARISON-INVARIANTS`, `R-V143-SLICE2A-FORWARD-RETURN-BASIS-UNPINNED`): Slice 2a's raw classifier logic is directionally sound, but the weekly comparison record contract is not commit-safe as evidence-clock infrastructure. (`R-V143-SLICE2A-COMPARISON-INVARIANTS`) `build_comparison_record()` currently emits self-consistent `divergence`, `forward_returns_pending`, and `backfill_complete`, but `schemas/a_short_regime_comparison_weekly.schema.json` accepts contradictory artifacts: equal `v14_2_regime`/`v14_3_raw_regime` with `divergence=true`; non-null `h1` while `forward_returns_pending` still contains `h1`; all-null returns with `backfill_complete=true`; all-null returns with an empty pending list; and arbitrary `v14_2_regime` strings such as `nonsense_status`. That can corrupt the 12-week evidence clock or later switch review even if the initial builder is correct. Required repair: constrain what JSON Schema can constrain (at minimum `v14_2_regime` enum matching production statuses, boundary already const) and add producer/backfill-side consistency validation with adversarial tests for divergence, pending/null symmetry, backfill_complete, and fired-rule/regime consistency where schema cannot compare fields. (`R-V143-SLICE2A-FORWARD-RETURN-BASIS-UNPINNED`) the schema calls `h1/h3/h5/h10` "forward excess return" but does not pin benchmark, unit, price basis, universe/portfolio scope, or cost/slippage basis. Future backfill can mix raw index returns, candidate returns, CSI300/CSI1000 excess, percentage points, or decimals while still validating. Required repair: add an explicit `forward_return_basis` contract (or const-pinned governance reference) defining unit and measurement basis before any record is used for switch-candidate evidence; tests must reject missing/mismatched basis. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** (`R-V143-SLICE2A-COMPARISON-INVARIANTS`) added `validate_comparison_record()` enforcing the cross-field invariants JSON Schema cannot — `divergence==(v14_2≠v14_3_raw)`, `forward_returns_pending` equals exactly the null horizons, `backfill_complete==(no nulls)`, `v14_3_fired_rule` is a rule of `v14_3_raw_regime` (single-source `FIRED_RULES_BY_REGIME`); `build_comparison_record` now self-validates before returning and rejects an out-of-enum `v14_2_regime`. Schema tightened: `v14_2_regime` enum `{attack,shock,defense,contraction,unknown}` (mirrors production `market_regime.status`), `v14_3_fired_rule` enum, `forward_returns_pending` uniqueItems. Adversarial tests added for all five contradiction probes (equal-regime+divergence-true, non-null-in-pending, all-null+backfill-true, all-null+empty-pending, nonsense v14_2_regime) + fired-rule/regime mismatch, validated at both validator and schema layers. (`R-V143-SLICE2A-FORWARD-RETURN-BASIS-UNPINNED`) added const-pinned `forward_return_basis` (required field, all subfields `const` in schema + `FORWARD_RETURN_BASIS` in code): CSI1000 `000852.SH`, forward close-to-close simple return, horizons 1/3/5/10 trading days, unit=percent, index_close_unadjusted, gross, market-level regime indicator; validator + schema reject missing/mismatched basis (decimal-unit and missing-basis probes tested). Verification: `tests.test_a_short_regime_classifier` 40/40 (+11 invariant/basis tests) + governance + route-doc = 68/68 OK.

A-short V14.3 slice 2a raw-classifier + slice 2b cadence design decision (status `slice2a resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master; **slice2b cadence/ledger decision remains `open` by design**; severity `P2`; scope `engine/a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `schemas/a_short_regime_comparison_weekly.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_regime_classifier.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md` (§6 split), `docs/README.md`; PIT label `comparison-only regime raw classifier / weekly comparison record / 252d-percentile cadence feasibility`): Slice 2a delivers the PURE per-day raw classifier (`classify_raw_regime`) + weekly comparison record (`build_comparison_record`) + schema + 29 tests — comparison-only, no data fetch, no EGS wiring, no production, no state machine (slice 3). Faithful to slice-1 governance const thresholds (percentile resolvers parity-tested against the const strings; consecutive-day operands break on null; attack requires all 6 operands, any null blocks it; insufficient-252d-window and missing-CSI1000 flagged). **Open design decision carried to slice 2b (NOT resolved here):** the 252d rolling-percentile thresholds need a daily-feature history, but the EGS run is weekly and produces one row per run — so breadth percentiles cannot be computed in-run from a single day. Proposed resolution: a persisted **incremental daily-feature ledger** in the a_short lane (one-time 252-trading-day backfill, then ~5 new days appended per weekly run; percentiles read from the ledger), rather than recomputing 252×(daily+stk_limit) calls every run. This ledger is a new managed state file and must be specified + reviewed before slice 2b wires the in-EGS producer + panel comparison-only block. Also noted: production `market_context.market_regime.status` is currently `unknown` (EGS does not compute V14.2 M1), so the comparison record's `v14_2_regime` will often be `unknown` (contract already allows it; divergence stats stay meaningful). No production / Phase 5 / veto / overlay / M6.7 behavior changed.

A-short V14.3 residual missing-index flag schema gap (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master; severity `P1`; scope `schemas/a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_v14_3_regime_governance.py`, `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md`, `presets/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance_20260611.json`; PIT label `comparison-only regime daily feature contract / missing index data honesty`; Required ID `R-V143-REGIME-MISSING-INDEX-FLAG-NOT-ENFORCED`): The repair for `R-V143-REGIME-SLOW-BLEED-FIELD-GAP` correctly added `csi1000_below_ma20` as the explicit slow-bleed operand and documents that missing index data must be represented as `csi1000_below_ma20=null` plus `data_quality_flags` containing `csi1000_unavailable`. However this is still only prose: a row with `csi1000_below_ma20=null` and empty `data_quality_flags` validates against `schemas/a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json`. That leaves Slice 2 free to emit ambiguous missing-index rows while still passing the advertised schema-first contract. Required repair: add a schema-level conditional (or an equivalent adversarial test plus producer-facing contract if JSON Schema support is intentionally limited) so `csi1000_below_ma20: null` requires `data_quality_flags` to contain `csi1000_unavailable`; add a negative test proving null-without-flag is rejected and a positive test proving null-with-flag is accepted. Keep the comparison-only / no-production boundary unchanged. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree; closure requires Codex re-`审查` PASS + commit):** added a top-level `if/then` to `schemas/a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json` — when `csi1000_below_ma20` is `null`, `data_quality_flags` is required and must `contain` `csi1000_unavailable`; null-without-flag now fails validation. Tests added: `test_null_csi1000_requires_unavailable_flag` (negative), `test_null_csi1000_with_flag_validates` (positive), `test_nonnull_csi1000_does_not_require_flag` (conditional does not over-fire). Design §2 notes the schema-enforced constraint. Boundary unchanged (comparison-only / non-production). Verification: `tests.test_a_short_v14_3_regime_governance` 28/28 + route-doc guard 14/14 OK.

A-short V14.3 regime classifier design/schema blockers (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS + committed local master; severity `P1`; scope `docs/a_short_v14_3_regime_classifier_design_20260611.md`, `schemas/a_short_market_regime_daily.schema.json`, `presets/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance_20260611.json`, `schemas/a_short_v14_3_regime_governance.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_short_v14_3_regime_governance.py`, `docs/README.md`; PIT label `comparison-only market-regime classifier / schema-first governance / future evidence clock`; Required IDs `R-V143-REGIME-SLOW-BLEED-FIELD-GAP`, `R-V143-REGIME-GOVERNANCE-CONST-PIN-GAP`): Codex review of the schema-first V14.3 proposal found the comparison-only boundary itself is directionally sound, but the slice is not commit-safe. (`R-V143-REGIME-SLOW-BLEED-FIELD-GAP`) The design's contraction slow-bleed rule requires `pct_above_ma20<30%` for 5 days **and** `csi1000_close < csi1000_ma20`, but the daily feature contract only carries `csi1000_ret_1d` and `pct_above_ma20`; no `csi1000_close`, `csi1000_ma20`, or boolean `csi1000_below_ma20` is required. Slice 2 therefore cannot implement the documented rule from the schema without adding an unstated data dependency or silently dropping half the slow-bleed condition. Required repair: add the CSI1000 MA20 state to the daily schema/design/governance/test fixture (or remove/rewrite the rule so every operand is represented), and pin null/quality-flag behavior for missing index data. (`R-V143-REGIME-GOVERNANCE-CONST-PIN-GAP`) The governance schema claims to pin metric definitions, thresholds, action matrix, and switch gate, but those areas are mostly unconstrained `object`s: a mutation changing `attack_all_of_confirm_3d.promotion_rate_ge` to a broken string and removing `switch_candidate_gate.backtest_years_min` still validates against the schema. Current tests only catch a subset (boundary and the low-risk ceiling string), so threshold/action/gate drift can pass the advertised schema-first contract. Required repair: make the governance schema or tests actually pin the frozen keys and key values required by the design, including threshold formulas, documented-only action matrix status, `backtest_years_min=2`, `divergence_samples_min=8`, no-auto-switch, and the review/no-fluke/no-missed-opportunity gate booleans. Do not commit the V14.3 slice until repaired and re-reviewed. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree):** (`R-V143-REGIME-SLOW-BLEED-FIELD-GAP`) added required `csi1000_below_ma20` (boolean|null) to the daily schema; design §2/§3 + governance slow_bleed now reference it (operand represented); null + `csi1000_unavailable` flag pinned for missing index data; test `test_daily_requires_csi1000_below_ma20`. (`R-V143-REGIME-GOVERNANCE-CONST-PIN-GAP`) rewrote the governance schema to **const-pin** every frozen value (defense/contraction/attack threshold formulas+anchors incl. the ceiling `min(50,max(P25_252,10))`, shock residual, state-machine days, action-matrix `_status` documented-only, switch gate `forward_live_weeks_min=12`/`backtest_years_min=2`/`divergence_samples_min=8`/`auto_switch_allowed=false`+review booleans, comparison-only boundary) with `additionalProperties:false`; adversarial tests prove a broken `promotion_rate_ge`, a removed `backtest_years_min`, a flipped `auto_switch_allowed`, and a flipped `production_switch_authorized` are all rejected. Verification: `tests.test_a_short_v14_3_regime_governance` 25/25 (incl. mutation-rejection) + route-doc OK; governance validates against the tightened schema; all JSON valid.

A-short Slice A overlay in-EGS wiring governance / mode-boundary drift (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS, committed in this overlay-wiring commit; severity `P1`; scope `A-EGS/egs_main.py`, `runners/a_short_theme_overlay_comparison.py`, `presets/a_short_theme_overlay_governance_20260610.json`, `docs/README.md`, and the overlay tests; PIT label `comparison artifact governance / L3 source boundary / cross-LLM route state`; Required IDs `R-ASHORT-OVERLAY-GOVERNANCE-RUNTIME-DRIFT`, `R-ASHORT-OVERLAY-L3-MODE-BOUNDARY-DRIFT`): Codex review on 2026-06-11 found the functional overlay/panel tests pass, but the current slice is not commit-safe because its runtime boundary and governance state contradict the actual wiring. The implementation now adds an `egs_main.py` side-output block that writes `overlay.json` from in-memory EGS data, but the governance artifact still states `scope.egs_main_runtime_changed=false`, the runner docstring still describes the overlay runner as a read-only consumer that does not modify `egs_main.py`, and `docs/README.md` says both "does NOT modify egs_main.py" and "Data-loading WIRED inside the EGS run" in the same current route row. Separately, the current comments/docs say only `--l3-mode pit` runs emit overlay, but the code does not check `CONF["l3_mode"]`; default `today` mode can write a same-day L3 snapshot in `score_l3()` and the overlay block then calls `_load_l3_snapshot(TODAY)`, so a default today-mode EGS run can also emit `overlay.json` while still recording `pit_source={"concept_membership":"pit"}`. Required repair: make the boundary explicit and machine-tested. Either (a) if overlay must be pit-only, add an explicit `CONF["l3_mode"] == "pit"` gate before building overlay and add a regression test proving today/neutralize skip; or (b) if today-mode overlay is allowed, update the route docs/governance/schema or emitted provenance so the artifact honestly records the source mode/snapshot date and no longer claims pit-only behavior. In both cases update `presets/a_short_theme_overlay_governance_20260610.json`, `docs/README.md`, and `runners/a_short_theme_overlay_comparison.py` so they no longer claim `egs_main` was untouched / runtime unchanged, while preserving the important boundary that production `final_score` / `tier` / admission are unchanged. Do not commit the overlay wiring until this is repaired and re-reviewed. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree):** chose option (a) — **pit-only, machine-tested**. (`R-ASHORT-OVERLAY-L3-MODE-BOUNDARY-DRIFT`) added pure `overlay_emit_allowed(l3_mode)` (= `l3_mode=='pit'`); egs_main gates `_load_l3_snapshot` on it (`... if overlay_emit_allowed(CONF.get("l3_mode")) else None`), so today/neutralize/None skip; test `test_overlay_emit_gated_to_pit_mode` proves pit→True, today/neutralize/None/''→False, so the emitted `pit_source=pit` label is honest. (`R-ASHORT-OVERLAY-GOVERNANCE-RUNTIME-DRIFT`) governance scope now `egs_main_runtime_changed=true` + new `egs_main_runtime_change_note` + `egs_main_production_behavior_changed=false` (production scoring untouched); README route row rewritten ("does NOT change egs_main PRODUCTION scoring … but DOES add a non-production overlay side-output"); test `test_governance_egs_main_boundary_honest` asserts runtime_changed=true ∧ production_behavior_changed=false ∧ production_ranking_changed=false. Optional also done: `test_main_writes_markdown_sibling_with_banner` (pipeline main writes `weekly_m67.md` sibling with the honesty banner). Verification: overlay + weekly + render + run-paths + phase6 + route-doc 88/88 OK; `py_compile` OK; `replacement_chars=0`. **Repair round 2 (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11):** Codex re-review found the governance/README honest but the **overlay runner module docstring** still claimed "不修改 `egs_main.py`/不改 production…/不新抓数据 + read-only consumer". Rewrote the module docstring to the honest boundary (slice DOES add a non-production `egs_main` side-output via `build_overlay_summary_from_panels`; does NOT change production `final_score`/`tier`/admission; no new fetch). Re-grep `不修改.*egs_main` / `does NOT modify egs_main`: zero source hits (the weekly-pipeline docstring's "不动 egs_main" is accurate — the pipeline itself does not modify egs_main; only `.pyc` caches + this finding's description remain). 34/34 + py_compile OK; module `replacement_chars=0`.

A-short per-run bundle convention not wired into the actual weekly flow (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex re-`审查` PASS, committed in this run-bundle commit; severity `P1`; scope `runners/weekly_screening.ps1`, `runners/forward_tracker.py`, `A-EGS/egs_main.py`, `docs/CURRENT.md`, `docs/a_short_run_bundle_convention_20260611.md`, `tests/test_a_short_run_paths.py` or `tests/phase6/test_weekly_screening_guardrails.py`; PIT label `run artifact layout / weekly execution entrypoint`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-RUNBUNDLE-NOT-WIRED-TO-WEEKLY-FLOW`): Codex review on 2026-06-11 found the new path helper and docs define the desired folder `research/results/a_short/<as_of>/`, but the actual common run path still does not use it. `runners/weekly_screening.ps1` invokes EGS without `--output-root research/results/a_short`, `docs/CURRENT.md` still advertises `python A-EGS\egs_main.py --as-of <YYYYMMDD>`, and `runners/forward_tracker.py` still reads `result/a_short/<as_of>/analysis_input.json`. Meanwhile `A-EGS/egs_main.py` writes only the side comparison artifact through `weight_comparison_path(TODAY)`, ignoring `run_egs(output_root=...)`. Result: a user following the existing weekly/common command still gets `analysis_input/candidates/snapshot` in the old `result/a_short/<as_of>/` location while `egs_weight_comparison.json` goes to the new bundle, and the tracker cannot read the new bundle if the documented manual command is used. This fails the user's requirement that each run's selection and analysis artifacts live in one easy-to-find folder. Required repair: wire the consolidated bundle path into the sanctioned execution entrypoint(s) or add a reviewed wrapper that does so, update downstream readers such as forward_tracker (or explicitly preserve a legacy/default boundary without claiming "each run"), make `egs_main` side outputs derive from the same run output root when applicable, and add regression tests proving the weekly/common path produces/reads one `research/results/a_short/<as_of>/` bundle instead of splitting artifacts. Do not commit the convention as a completed solution until this is repaired and re-reviewed. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-11, working tree):** reframed the convention to **"a run's bundle = `<its EGS output_root>/<as_of>/`"** and made egs_main's comparison-diff **derive from the run's `output_root`** (`weight_comparison_path(TODAY, output_root=output_root)`) so it ALWAYS co-locates with `analysis_input` (path module now mirrors `export_analysis_input`'s base_root resolution). Two flows made explicit (the chosen "preserve legacy boundary" option, no "every run in research/results" claim): **production** (default output-root → `result/a_short/<as_of>/`; `forward_tracker`/`weekly_screening.ps1`/CURRENT command UNCHANGED, comparison now co-locates there) vs **analysis** (`--output-root research/results/a_short` → bundle + weekly M6.7 there, guard-safe). Regression tests added: `test_comparison_colocates_with_analysis_input_any_output_root` (no split for any output_root), `test_analysis_flow_bundle_is_guard_safe`, `test_production_bundle_matches_forward_tracker_read_root` (`run_bundle_dir(as_of)` == `forward_tracker.LIVE_RESULT_ROOT/as_of`). Convention doc + README updated. Verification: run-paths + weekly + egs + phase6 loadability 72/72 OK; `py_compile` OK; register `replacement_chars=0`.

A-short production EGS industry_heat promotion v1 — implementation slice (status `resolved` 2026-06-11 Codex `审查` PASS + committed `3494abd`; severity `P1`; scope `engine/egs_industry_heat.py`, `A-EGS/egs_main.py`, `presets/egs_industry_heat_governance_20260611.json`, `schemas/egs_industry_heat_governance.schema.json`, `schemas/analysis_input.schema.json`, `tests/test_egs_industry_heat.py`, `docs/a_short_egs_industry_heat_promotion_design_20260611.md`; PIT label `production selection scoring / governed SW L2 industry beta`; Required IDs across rounds `R-EGS-INDHEAT-ACTIVE-PROFILE-NOT-PRODUCTION-PROMOTION`, `R-EGS-INDHEAT-GOVERNANCE-PROFILE-DRIFT`, `R-EGS-INDHEAT-DEFINITION-DRIFT`, `R-EGS-INDHEAT-STALE-LEGACY-DOC-LOG`, `R-EGS-INDHEAT-DEF-SCHEMA-UNPINNED`, `R-EGS-INDHEAT-STALE-LEGACY-AND-OVERLAY-WORDING`, `R-EGS-INDHEAT-RISK-REGISTER-ENCODING-CORRUPTION`; optional `R-EGS-WEIGHT-COMPARISON-ASOF-GAP`): implements the design forward-item below as a live v1. Scoring tail extracted to single-source pure module `engine/egs_industry_heat.py` (egs_main + the per-run non-production weight-comparison diff both call it; no separate runner). **`active_profile=balanced` is LIVE** (esp.20/cat.25/l4.40/industry_heat.15) → raises 行业/赛道 0.30→0.40 and changes production selection (user-directed); `legacy` (esp.20/cat.30/l4.50/ind 0) retained as one-flag rollback + regression anchor. industry_heat = production-pinned per-SW-L2 median-momentum cross-industry percentile (adapts the overlay percentile CONCEPT, not a literal reuse; unknown L2 → null; no L1 fallback v1); ranking-only — never rescues hard_veto/停牌/涨停锁/ST/减持/闪崩; chasing_high·overheat·unknown-industry → Tier2 preserved; l4 industry kickers left intact (mild documented overlap, l4_score untouched). Governance schema **const-pins every profile's 4 weights AND `industry_heat_def`** (rejects drift / deletion / fake future-return def). Per-run artifact `<EGS output_root>/<as_of>/egs_weight_comparison.json` (co-located with analysis_input in the run bundle per `docs/a_short_run_bundle_convention_20260611.md`; updated 2026-06-11 from the original flat research/results path to the run bundle) carries `as_of` + `legacy_vs` diff + `variant_top_n` (each profile's top-N, labeled comparison-only / NOT tradeable). Repair rounds fixed: active-profile not promoting (→ balanced live), profile/def drift (→ const-pin + adversarial tests), definition over-claim (→ honest production-pinned wording), stale legacy-default doc/log/test/module wording (→ balanced-live everywhere; removed the nonexistent-runner reference), and **risk-register UTF-8 replacement-char corruption (restored from HEAD baseline + re-applied edits cleanly; replacement_total back to 0)**. Verification: `tests.test_egs_industry_heat` 21/21; broad regression 80/80; route-doc guard 14/14; `py_compile` OK. **Committed `3494abd` (Codex `审查` PASS).** Only the forward-return scoreboard + promotion auto-flag (needs ≥12wk forward data) + the `theme_double` condition-triggered promotion remain open — tracked by the forward-item below (P2).

A-short production EGS 行业/赛道 weight promotion — design forward-item (status `open`; severity `P2` roadmap forward-item, NOT a current blocker; scope FUTURE reviewed change to `A-EGS/egs_main.py` egs_base scoring + `presets/` governance + `schemas/analysis_input.schema.json` + EGS CSV output + parity/adversarial tests + Slice A overlay industry_heat concept adaptation (production-pinned, not literal reuse); PIT label `production selection scoring / SW L2 industry beta`; origin: user-directed 2026-06-11, Codex-refined): user (approver) directed raising 行业/赛道 importance in the PRODUCTION selection scoring (not only the analysis/overlay layer), rationale = sector beta (hot 赛道 lifts the whole SW L2 industry). **Locked v1 production default formula** (IMPLEMENTED + LIVE in the implementation slice above; this forward-item now tracks only the still-open forward-evaluation parts): `egs_base = esp 0.20 + cat 0.25 + l4 0.40 + industry_heat 0.15` (was `esp 0.20 + cat 0.30 + l4 0.50` at `egs_main.py:2743`); new `industry_heat_score` = SW L2 industry beta, weight taken from cat(-0.05)+l4(-0.10), **esp 0.20 kept (fundamentals anchor)**. Constraints: industry heat ADDS to ranking only — never rescues hard_veto/停牌/涨停锁/ST/减持/闪崩; keep `chasing_high·overheat → Tier2` (egs_main:2778-2781); industry_heat is a production-pinned per-SW-L2 median-momentum cross-industry percentile that ADAPTS the overlay percentile concept (NOT a literal reuse; no L1 fallback v1); avoid double-counting l4's existing industry kickers (`ind_mom_cnt≥3 +5`, industry-not-falling +10 at egs_main:2601/2614) — remove or quantify overlap in parity tests; the drafted slice MUST emit a "new vs current" selection diff (Tier1 membership + overheat-name share) so activation is not blind. **Deferred condition-triggered variant — 概念+行业双提 (esp.15/cat.35/l4.35/ind.15): NOT scheduled, do NOT rely on memory.** It is promoted ONLY if, over a rolling ≥12-week window, the comparison-track shows (a) its would-be Tier1 forward 1–3wk return beats the balanced default by a pre-set noise-passing margin AND (b) its selected-set overheat/chasing share + realized drawdown are not materially worse, then (c) via a reviewed governance change; otherwise it stays logged forever, never auto-promoted. **Anti-forget requirement on the future slice:** the comparison-track must (1) log all 3 variants (balanced default / aggressive / 双提) weekly Tier1 selection + forward return, (2) auto-raise a "variant = promotion candidate" flag when the trigger fires, (3) keep this forward-item open until the slice bakes the scoreboard + flag in with tests. Closes only when the production weight change + variant scoreboard + auto-flag ship reviewed, or the user explicitly drops it. Does NOT block batch ② or any current work.

A-short weekly pipeline batch ② Required repair closeout (status `resolved` 2026-06-10 Codex re-`审查` PASS; severity `P0/P1 closeout`; scope `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `runners/a_short_phase5_engine.py`, `tests/test_a_short_phase5_engine.py`, `docs/README.md`, `docs/a_short_weekly_pipeline_design_20260610.md`, `schemas/a_short_weekly_report.schema.json`; PIT label `weekly report / batch ② official-output integrity`; Required IDs `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-IV-FEED-PIT-CROSS-ASOF`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-PRICE-FETCH-FAIL-OPEN`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-EGS-HARD-RISK-MAPPING-GAP`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-OFFICIAL-OUTPUT-PATH-BOUNDARY`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-OVERLAY-CONSUMER-VALIDATION-GAP`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-ANALYSIS-INPUT-CONSUMER-VALIDATION-GAP`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-OFFICIAL-OUTPUT-ROOT-MISMATCH`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-WRITE-ASOF-CALENDAR-GAP`, `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-PRICE-SERIES-PIT-FRESHNESS-GAP`): Codex final re-review on 2026-06-10 verified all nine Required findings in the A-short batch ② weekly pipeline slice are repaired in the working tree. The final price-series repair validates provider `trade_date` values as real calendar dates, rejects future rows, requires latest fetched bar to equal `--as-of`, and aborts without writing on violation; independent probes confirmed future/stale/non-calendar price rows and fake-`tushare` future rows no longer write. The prior repairs also hold: IV feed cross_as_of is rejected, required price coverage fail-open is closed, real EGS hard-risk fields map into hard veto behavior, production-shaped `result/a_short/...` output paths are refused under the narrowed caller-controlled output contract, overlay artifacts are schema/consistency/as_of validated, `analysis_input` is validated through the repo contract with `trade_date == --as-of`, and empty weekly reports with invalid calendar `as_of` are rejected. Verification: targeted A-short batch suite 182/182 OK, route-doc guard included; `py_compile` OK; `git diff --check` OK before review-log edits; full `unittest discover -v` still reports the known six import-time `A-EGS/egs_main.py` environment errors outside this slice. This closeout supersedes the detailed batch ② `in_progress` entries below and authorizes committing this reviewed slice only; it does not authorize live Tushare execution, provider fetch expansion, production `result/a_short` writes, ship-gate evidence, real-money sizing, DataHub work, or broker/order automation.

A-short weekly pipeline price-series PIT / freshness validation gap (status `superseded` by the batch ② Required-repair closeout above (committed `89a1318`, Codex PASS); severity `P0`; scope `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`; PIT label `weekly report / required stock price coverage and PIT freshness`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-PRICE-SERIES-PIT-FRESHNESS-GAP`): Codex re-review on 2026-06-10 found `_fetch_price_series()` sorts provider rows and returns only `{high, low, close}`; it never validates provider `trade_date` as a real calendar date, never rejects rows after `end` / weekly `as_of`, and never requires the latest returned bar to match the run `as_of`. Independent probes showed `_fetch_price_series(..., end=20260609)` accepts a future `trade_date=20260630` row and includes its `high=99.0 / low=1.0 / close=50.0`; it also accepts a 30-row series whose latest dates are all in `202601xx`. A fake `tushare` module through `main(... --confirm-fetch-authorized)` wrote `weekly.json` in both the future-row and stale-row cases. Required repair: validate every fetched `trade_date` as real `YYYYMMDD`, reject provider rows with `trade_date > end`, require latest fetched bar to equal `analysis_input.trade_date / --as-of` for included candidates (or document and test an explicit reviewed tolerance), abort without writing on violation, and add adversarial tests for future rows and stale latest bars. Blocks committing / executing batch ②. **Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-10, working tree)**: `_fetch_price_series()` now validates every `trade_date` as a real calendar date, rejects any `trade_date > end` (future bar), and requires the latest bar to equal `end` (== `--as-of`; chose exact-match over a tolerance), aborting via `SystemExit` without writing on any violation; still returns `{high,low,close}` engine shape. Tests: `test_future_bar_aborts`, `test_stale_latest_bar_aborts`, `test_noncalendar_trade_date_aborts`, `test_main_future_price_row_writes_no_file` (fake `tushare` through `main(--confirm-fetch-authorized)` → SystemExit, no file), and `test_uses_stock_asset_E_and_returns_bars` (latest==end happy path). Verification: A-short batch suite 170/170 OK (weekly file 41 tests); route-doc guard 14/14 incl.; `py_compile` OK.

A-short weekly pipeline price fetch fail-open and wrong asset channel (status `superseded` by the batch ② Required-repair closeout above (committed `89a1318`, Codex PASS); severity `P0`; scope `runners/a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`, `tests/test_a_short_weekly_pipeline.py`; PIT label `weekly report / required stock price coverage`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-WEEKLY-PRICE-FETCH-FAIL-OPEN`): Codex review on 2026-06-10 found `_fetch_price_series()` calls Tushare `pro_bar(... asset="FD")` for stock codes, while installed Tushare `pro_bar` documents/defaults `asset="E"` for stocks/exchange funds and `FD` for funds. Blocks committing / executing batch ②.

A-short IV feed build fetch window cannot produce rolling percentile (status `resolved` 2026-06-10 Codex re-`审查` PASS; severity `P0`; scope `runners/a_short_iv_feed_build.py`, `runners/a_short_iv_feed_probe.py`, `tests/test_a_short_iv_feed_build.py`; PIT label `IV feed execution / 252d percentile availability`; Required ID `R-ASHORT-IVFEED-BUILD-FETCH-WINDOW-NO-PERCENTILE`): Codex review on 2026-06-10 found batch ① IV-feed build reused the probe fetcher while it truncated trade dates to 25, making the 60-observation minimum impossible. A-short IV feed probe latest-usable summary consistency gap (status `resolved` 2026-06-10 Claude `审查` PASS; severity `P1`; scope `schemas/a_short_iv_feed_probe_summary.schema.json`, `runners/a_short_iv_feed_probe.py`, `tests/test_a_short_iv_feed_probe.py`, `docs/a_short_iv_feed_design_20260610.md`; PIT label `IV-feed probe official summary contract / latest usable valuation date`; Required ID `R-AIV-PROBE-SUMMARY-LATEST-USABLE-CONSISTENCY`): Codex re-review on 2026-06-10 found the runner-side as_of and latest-usable-date repair fixed the prior P0 false positives, but the official summary contract still accepts contradictory `computable=true` summaries. Closure still requires review/commit. Do not commit this probe slice, run a Tushare probe, fetch option data, build the IV feed, wire Slice B, change production scoring, claim IV coverage, ship-gate evidence, real-money readiness, or use IV status in manual trading until this Required item receives repair + independent review PASS.

Second, the probe returns `computable=true` when the latest common PIT date has zero valid option quotes: valid quotes on only the first 15 of 20 common PIT days satisfy `valid_quote_days`, while `spot_ref` is taken from the latest common date and ATM bracketing uses valid quote contracts collected across the whole window. Required repair: validate `as_of` as a real YYYYMMDD calendar date in producer and schema/consistency layers; require the latest usable PIT valuation date to have valid option quotes and the two quotable future maturities needed for that date; compute ATM bracketing from contracts quoted on that latest usable valuation date, not stale contracts seen anywhere in the window; add adversarial tests for invalid calendar `as_of` and latest-common-date zero quotes / stale valid-code bracketing. A-short Slice A theme-overlay runner eligibility / crowding / schema contract gaps (status `resolved`; severity `P0`; scope `runners/a_short_theme_overlay_comparison.py`, `schemas/a_short_theme_overlay_comparison.schema.json`, `presets/a_short_theme_overlay_governance_20260610.json`, `tests/test_a_short_theme_overlay_comparison.py`, `tests/schema/test_a_short_theme_overlay_comparison_schema.py`; PIT label `comparison-track scoring/schema contract / no market-data PIT`; Required IDs `R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-ELIGIBILITY-BONUS-GATE`, `R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-CROWDING-HARD-GATE`, `R-ASLICEA-SCHEMA-OUTPUT-INVARIANTS`): Codex review on 2026-06-10 found the uncommitted Slice A runner/schema slice is not commit-safe or execution-safe. `R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-ELIGIBILITY-BONUS-GATE`: the committed Slice A design requires at least 2 of `theme_heat / industry_heat / breadth` plus `fit_pass` before any theme/industry bonus; otherwise overlay must be `esp+l4` only. The runner computes `eligible`, but gates `theme_eff` and `industry_term` only on `fit_pass`; an independent probe produced `eligible=false` with an extra `35.0` points of theme/industry bonus. `R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-CROWDING-HARD-GATE`: the design invariant says hard-veto/crowding means heat bonus cannot rescue the candidate, but current crowding handling only multiplies the already-bonused `overlay_raw` by `0.5`, and tests do not prove high heat cannot rescue. `R-ASLICEA-SCHEMA-OUTPUT-INVARIANTS`: the schema allows unpinned threshold-like extras under `thresholds` and does not enforce `candidate_count == len(candidates)`; an independent probe showed both mutations still validate. Required repair: use a single bonus gate equivalent to `eligible` (plus any clarified hard-veto/crowding risk-family condition) for both theme and industry terms; add high-heat adversarial tests for ineligible and crowding/hard-veto cases; close or const-pin every emitted threshold/governance key; add producer-side summary consistency checks for candidate count and rank uniqueness/contiguity with adversarial tests. Do not commit this runner slice, wire data loading, run overlay comparison, fetch data, modify `egs_main.py` / production `final_score` / `tier` / admission, claim production / ship-gate / buy-advice readiness, or use the overlay for manual trading until these Required items receive repair + independent review PASS. Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-10): introduced one `bonus_gate = eligible ∧ ¬crowding_hit` (`eligible = fit_pass ∧ ≥2 of theme/industry/breadth`); both theme and industry terms are zeroed unless `bonus_gate`, so an ineligible row scores `esp+l4` base (R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-ELIGIBILITY-BONUS-GATE). Crowding now STRIPS the heat bonus (removed the `×0.5` demote) so a crowded high-heat row scores exactly base — heat cannot rescue it (R-ASLICEA-RUNNER-CROWDING-HARD-GATE). Schema `thresholds` is now `additionalProperties:false` with every key const-pinned incl. `theme_window_blend`, and `validate_overlay_summary_consistency` enforces `candidate_count == len(candidates)` + unique-contiguous `overlay_rank` (R-ASLICEA-SCHEMA-OUTPUT-INVARIANTS). Design doc §2 + governance `thresholds` aligned to the runner. Verification: overlay unit+schema 28/28, route-doc guard 14/14, py_compile OK; independent mutation probes pass. Comparison-track / non-production / no fetch / V14.2 frozen unchanged. Codex re-`审查` PASS 2026-06-10 (no Required; two wording-only Optionals fixed at commit). A-short Slice A theme-overlay computation contract gaps (status `resolved`; severity `P1`; scope `docs/a_short_theme_overlay_slice_a_design_20260610.md` and future Slice A runner/governance/schema tests; PIT label `overlay scoring contract / no market-data PIT`; Required IDs `R-ASLICEA-INDUSTRY-ORTHO-SCALE`, `R-ASLICEA-FIT-GATE-CONSISTENCY`): Codex review on 2026-06-10 found the Slice A design is not commit-safe yet. `R-ASLICEA-INDUSTRY-ORTHO-SCALE`: the spec defines `industry_heat_score` as 0-100, then says `industry_heat⊥` is a regression residual used directly in `overlay_raw` with a 0.15 weight, but does not define whether the residual is re-ranked / clipped / shifted back onto the same 0-100 scale. This makes the frozen weight mathematically unstable and leaves the runner free to use raw residual units. Required repair: define the exact post-orthogonalization scale before weighting, preferably residualize cross-sectionally and then percentile-rank / normalize `industry_heat⊥` to 0-100, or explicitly choose a bounded exposure-cap method; add the corresponding output field / invariant wording for the runner sub-slice. `R-ASLICEA-FIT-GATE-CONSISTENCY`: the formula says theme/industry bonus is zero unless `fit != unknown` and `fit >= fit_floor`, but the "赛道红利资格门槛" only requires `fit != unknown`, and the output flags mention `fit_unknown` but not `fit_pass`. Required repair: make the gate one definition everywhere, e.g. `fit_pass = fit != unknown AND fit >= fit_floor`, use `fit_pass` for both theme and industry bonus eligibility, and include the flag in the overlay artifact/test checklist. Do not commit the Slice A design, build the runner, change EGS production scoring, run data/backtests, promote overlay ranking, claim production / ship-gate / full-size readiness, or change the parent design boundary until these receive independent review PASS. Closure requires a repaired design/log entry plus Codex re-review PASS; no market-data execution is involved in closing this entry. Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-10): `R-ASLICEA-INDUSTRY-ORTHO-SCALE` — residualize `industry_heat` on `theme_heat` cross-sectionally THEN percentile-normalize the residual back to 0–100 (`industry_heat_norm⊥ = pct_rank(residual)×100`); the weight formula now uses the normalized field, not the raw residual; added the output field + the §7 "theme⊥industry + 同尺度 / 禁止零中心残差加权" invariant. `R-ASLICEA-FIT-GATE-CONSISTENCY` — single `fit_pass=(fit≠unknown)∧(fit≥fit_floor)` defined once and reused in the weight formula (`fit_mult`), eligibility 门槛, output flags (added `fit_pass`), and §7 invariant; theme AND industry bonus both gate on it. Design-only; no frozen weight/threshold value changed (scale-normalization + condition-unification, not new numbers); overlay stays comparison-track non-production; V14.2 frozen. Codex re-`审查` PASS 2026-06-10 (no Required/Optional; route-doc guard 14/14). Resolved on Claude `提交` of the Slice A design repair (local master, no push); the runner sub-slice + governance/parity thresholds remain a future reviewed slice.

A-long large-cap batch signal-search runner coverage/audit gaps (status `resolved`; severity `P0`; scope `runners/a_long_large_cap_batch_factor_search_signal_search.py`, `schemas/a_long_large_cap_batch_factor_search_signal_search_execution_summary.schema.json`, `tests/test_a_long_large_cap_batch_factor_search_signal_search.py`, `tests/schema/test_a_long_large_cap_batch_factor_search_signal_search_schema.py`; PIT label `primary cohort construction / execution-summary diagnostics / no market-data PIT`; Required IDs `R-BATCH-RUNNER-SIZE-COVERAGE`, `R-BATCH-RUNNER-DIAGNOSTIC-AUDIT`): Codex review on 2026-06-09 found the new batch runner slice is not safe to commit or execute before two material gaps are fixed. `R-BATCH-RUNNER-SIZE-COVERAGE`: the preregistration freezes top-500 size buckets with `minimum_size_bucket_count_for_primary_percentile = 50` and says the size coverage gate applies to cohort-forming months, but the runner only skips scoring inside a thin bucket and still forms a primary cohort when the remaining scored names are >= 10. An independent probe with one missing size bucket and four healthy buckets still formed one primary cohort, which can contaminate the `industry_size_neutral` primary decision with a residual-size subset. Required repair: add per-factor/as-of size coverage diagnostics and hard cohort gating so any primary-hypothesis month whose actual factor observations or size-neutral scored observations have any market-cap quintile below 50 is excluded from that hypothesis's primary cohort and reported; add schema/consistency requirements and adversarial tests proving a missing/thin bucket cannot form a primary cohort. `R-BATCH-RUNNER-DIAGNOSTIC-AUDIT`: the preregistration requires sorted BH-FDR primary p-values and per-factor input-coverage counts, but the summary currently records only thresholds / FDR booleans and lacks separate missing / non-positive / insufficient-TTM counters for the value factors (`book_to_circ_mv`, `cash_flow_to_circ_mv`, `sales_to_circ_mv`). Required repair: emit a schema-required sorted BH audit table (factor_id, primary_cell_id, p_value, rank, q=0.10 / q=0.05 thresholds, survives flags) and per-factor raw-input exclusion / availability counters for all nine factors, with adversarial schema/internal-consistency tests. Do not commit this runner slice, run signal search, spend the singleton ledger, fetch data, change the frozen factor/q/universe/threshold design after seeing results, or claim alpha / production / ship-gate / full-size / DataHub / broker readiness before these receive independent review PASS. Repair (Claude `修复` 2026-06-09; Codex re-`审查` PASS 2026-06-09; pending Claude `提交`): `R-BATCH-RUNNER-SIZE-COVERAGE` — added a per-factor/per-as_of market-cap-quintile coverage pass in `monthly_cohort_rows` (counts each factor's RAW observations per quintile; a month with zero observations OR any quintile below `minimum_size_bucket_count_for_primary_percentile = 50` is recorded as a no-observation / incomplete-coverage month for that factor) and a HARD cohort gate: `cohort_excess_by_as_of` regained an `excluded_as_ofs` parameter and `summarize_results` excludes each factor's flagged months from its size-dependent views (`industry_size_neutral`, `size_neutral`); `validate_pipeline_result_sanity` now raises if any excluded month leaks into a primary cohort; a new `size_coverage_audit` summary section (per-factor observation / no-observation / incomplete / excluded month counts + lists + primary-cohort month count) is schema-required and consistency-checked. Independent re-probe basis: a month with one absent/thin quintile can no longer form that factor's primary cohort. `R-BATCH-RUNNER-DIAGNOSTIC-AUDIT` — `batch_factor_values` now records a per-factor raw-input outcome (`available` / `missing_input` / `non_positive` / `insufficient_ttm` / `insufficient_window`) for all nine factors (value factors get separate missing vs non-positive vs insufficient-TTM counts), surfaced as a schema-required `factor_input_coverage` section; and a schema-required `fdr_audit` section emits the sorted BH-FDR primary table (rank, factor_id, primary_cell_id, p_value, q=0.10 / q=0.05 BH critical values, survives flags) whose survival flags are consistency-checked against `factor_results`. Optional addressed: the composite primary score is now the family-equal-weight blend of already-neutral percentiles written directly (no re-neutralization). Verification: batch runner + schema tests 62/62 (was 52; +10 coverage/audit/leak/input tests incl. adversarial schema rejects of dropped coverage rows, count mismatches, dropped input-coverage factor, FDR survival disagreement, duplicate rank); a monkeypatched `build_summary`→schema structure test still validates the full emitted summary (now incl. the three new sections); full suite 1193/1193 (was 1183); route-doc guard 11/11; schema regenerated from the runner so the new-section pins match. Codex re-`审查` PASS 2026-06-09 (no Required, no Optional): independent probes confirmed an excluded `low_beta` month yields primary cohort count 0 while the non-size diagnostic keeps count 1, a planted FDR-audit/factor-results disagreement is rejected, the ledger is still unspent, and no execution summary exists; 62/62 batch + 11/11 route-doc + 1193/1193 full suite reproduced. - Evidence: `research/results/a_long_data_integrity_audit_20260603/audit_report.json` records the original `blocked_missing_required_source` result. The route was repaired through validation, gap repair, thin-slice materialization, broader materialization, daily route diagnostic, main-board-only scope guard, and the user-approved bounded `000666.SZ` delisted missing-industry exception. The follow-on preflight and SW repair established the 3,387-name main-board universe and the approved 191-name no-industry boundary. The old full raw materialization completed with 23,717 endpoint results, and the old local-only full audit passed with mandatory exclusions, but both artifacts are now stale under the latest amended gates. The amended path changed the materialization plan to 23,718 calls, adds PIT `namechange_2018_2025`, requires statement-table `f_ann_date`, locks `fina_indicator` to ann-date-only PIT with mandatory restatement exclusions, rejects checkpoint request-shape drift, points the audit benchmark check to H-code total-return close inputs, adds a hard PIT status-source audit check, and forbids current/final `stock_basic.name` as historical veto evidence. After commit `e6aa705`, the amended materialization runner passed shape with 23,718 endpoint results, 3 new Tushare calls, 23,715 reused payloads, and all 14 table rollups passed. After commit `bf16a7d`, the amended full audit passed with 12/12 self-tests, six check results pass/characterized, and zero network calls. After commit `1623761`, the pre-run ROE / veto / exit-policy repair passed review and was committed. After commit `498ab99`, the gate-reader repair passed review and was committed. After commit `2214648`, the first valid frozen signal search executed and wrote `research/results/a_long_signal_search_20260604/execution_summary.json` with `no_alpha_found_under_frozen_rules`, candidate count 0, 32 cells, 417,407 evaluated rows, clean hygiene, and full restatement-exclusion application. The ledger is spent as failed threshold.
- Accepted calibration: the fixed-panel route proof, old full raw materialization, and old repaired full audit are real historical data-readiness evidence, but they are not alpha evidence and no longer certify the amended signal runner. The 1,504 ambiguous same-ann-date groups must still be excluded from future signal inputs through `restatement_ambiguous_exclusions.csv`; the future signal runner must abort if the CSV is missing or unapplied. The total-return probe proves the existing account cannot support same-anchor total-return benchmark open, but it did prove close-only total-return rows for the preferred CSI series in the small probe. Raw stock price returns are not acceptable because split / bonus / transfer / rights events create signal-correlated discontinuities. Derived total-return-open construction and price-index fallback remain forbidden. Monthly long-horizon cohort count is not independent N, YTD cumulative fundamentals cannot be mixed across quarter lengths for earnings stability, and any selection-time ST / delisting-name veto must be PIT `namechange` based.
- Required next action: independent review of the real signal-search result summary, singleton ledger update, and post-execution test update. Do not rerun or rescue this failed A-long search. Any future A-long signal search requires a new reviewed preregistration and ledger update with no silent changes to families, thresholds, horizons, universe, cost model, benchmark policy, or industry policy.
- Closure requirement: the original data-route blocker closure condition is satisfied by the valid research-only summary plus singleton ledger update; after review, close this entry as a failed frozen alpha test, not as production / ship-gate / full-size evidence.

### SR-RESOURCE-001 - DataHub broad jobs lack code-level local resource budget enforcement

- Severity: P2
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: Phase 7c DataHub / local execution stability
- Evidence: `docs/datahub_design.md` accepts a future shared DataHub for four subsystems, but before this reviewed slice there was no contract requiring slice-first local execution, partitioned reads, lazy materialization, incremental cache reuse, checkpoint / resume, or explicit user approval before all-market / all-lane / full-refresh jobs.
- Accepted calibration: this is not proof that the user's machine cannot run the system. The intended operating mode does not require all four systems to run at once. The risk is future implementation drift: a DataHub or runner slice could accidentally make full-system refresh the default and overload local execution or make review / debugging unstable.
- Mitigation evidence: reviewed slices add `schemas/datahub_local_resource_budget.schema.json`, `docs/datahub_local_resource_budget_contract_20260602.json`, `schemas/datahub_job_spec.schema.json`, and `schemas/examples/datahub_job_spec.example.json`, and route them through `docs/datahub_design.md`, `docs/README.md`, `docs/CURRENT.md`, `AGENTS.md`, and the Phase 7 handoff. The contracts require default `single_slice_incremental` behavior, a declared job spec, market/lane consistency (`A` only with `a_*`, `US` only with `us_*`), reviewed `max_concurrent_lanes <= 2`, and blocks default all-system / all-market / all-lane / full-refresh runs.
- Closure evidence: `engine/datahub/job_spec_contract.py` now provides the code-level validation boundary for future DataHub / runner / report job specs. It validates the job spec against `schemas/datahub_job_spec.schema.json`, validates the referenced resource-budget contract against `schemas/datahub_local_resource_budget.schema.json`, checks the selected budget profile against the resource-budget artifact, enforces market/lane consistency, validates `as_of_date` inside a bounded date window, rejects `single_as_of` windows that are not exactly one day, requires reviewed executable jobs to have no blocking approval gates, keeps heavy profiles tied to explicit approval, and rejects provider calls, DataHub implementation authorization, runner changes, production-consumption claims, ship-gate claims, full-market refresh, all-lane runs, raw payload writes, and other scope creep.
- Required next action: no active `SR-RESOURCE-001` action. Every future Phase 7c DataHub table, adapter, runner, report job, or executable job spec must call `validate_datahub_job_spec_contract` / `validate_datahub_job_spec_file` before execution; if a future schema version intentionally authorizes broader work, update this validator and tests in the same reviewed slice.
- Verification: `tests.schema.test_datahub_local_resource_budget_schema` validates the resource-budget schema / artifact, default slice-first behavior, `max_concurrent_lanes <= 2`, budget profiles, implementation gates, and scope-creep rejection. `tests.schema.test_datahub_job_spec_schema` validates the job-spec schema / example, one-market / one-lane partitioning, market/lane mismatch rejection, resource estimates, execution policy locks, approval gates, and scope-creep rejection. `tests.schema.test_datahub_job_spec_contract` validates the runtime helper, reviewed executable job behavior, heavy approval checks, profile / resource-budget matching, date-window invariants, blocking-gate rejection, no-scope-creep enforcement, resource-budget lane-concurrency bounds, and non-mutation.

### SR-EXEC-001 - Historical weekly screening can contaminate official outputs

- Severity: P0
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: A-short operation / Phase 6b maintenance
- Evidence: `runners/weekly_screening.ps1` calls `A-EGS/egs_main.py --as-of $AsOf` without `--l3-mode`; `A-EGS/egs_main.py` defaults `--l3-mode` to `today` and writes official `result/a_short/<trade_date>/` outputs by default.
- Accepted calibration: `pit` lookup itself uses the effective as-of date after `set_asof`; the risk is the weekly script defaulting to `today` for historical `-AsOf` and allowing official-output overwrite.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set updates `runners/weekly_screening.ps1` so historical `-AsOf` runs must pass `-L3Mode pit` or `-L3Mode neutralize`, reject `-L3Mode today`, pass `--l3-pit-strict` for PIT mode, and refuse to overwrite existing `result/a_short/<AsOf>/` or `A-EGS/Result/egs_*_<AsOf>` official outputs without `-AllowHistoricalOverwrite`.
- Verification: `tests.phase6.test_weekly_screening_guardrails` covers missing historical L3 mode, rejected historical `today` mode, existing official-output overwrite refusal, and strict PIT argument wiring.

### SR-DATA-001 - Suspend inference can silently drop tradable stocks on partial daily response

- Severity: P1
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: A-short weekly operation / Phase 6b maintenance
- Evidence: `A-EGS/egs_main.py:get_suspend_info` computes `suspended = all_codes - traded_codes` from single-day `pro.daily` responses and later `filter_l0` removes that set from candidates. There is no row-count / completeness sanity check before treating missing `daily` rows as suspended stocks.
- Accepted calibration: this is a real wrong-output path, but the trigger is a partial `pro.daily` bulk response. Frequency is not proven and is expected to be low; impact is silent and high when it fires. It does not block corrected 5d revalidation if that revalidation uses frozen historical generated cohorts and does not rerun `A-EGS/egs_main.py`.
- Required next action: resolved for the suspend-inference path. No further action is required for `SR-DATA-001` unless the suspend source / daily completeness policy is redesigned.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set updates `A-EGS/egs_main.py:get_suspend_info` to validate non-empty `pro.daily` payloads against `suspend_daily_min_coverage = 0.95` before inferring `suspended = all_codes - traded_codes`; below-threshold partial responses now raise instead of silently filtering candidates. The suspend cache key is bumped to `suspend_<date>_v2` so old unvalidated suspend-inference caches are not reused.
- Verification: `tests.phase6.test_egs_main_suspend_guard` covers partial daily rejection, valid high-coverage daily inference, cache-key v2 save behavior, and the existing all-empty daily fallback that skips suspend filtering rather than marking the whole market suspended.

### SR-DATA-004 - Suspend daily completeness threshold may false-fail on large-scale suspension days

- Severity: P3
- Status: open
- Owner phase: A-short weekly operation / Phase 6b maintenance
- Evidence: `SR-DATA-001` resolved the partial-`daily` silent drop path by requiring non-empty `pro.daily` payload coverage to meet `suspend_daily_min_coverage = 0.95`. Claude review accepted the fail-safe behavior but noted the threshold assumes normal-day suspensions stay below 5%; rare market-stress days with unusually broad suspensions could push true traded-code coverage below 95% and abort the run even when the provider response is complete.
- Accepted calibration: this is a low-risk operational watch item, not evidence that normal weekly runs are broken. A clear abort is safer than silently inferring missing rows as suspended stocks, and `suspend_daily_min_coverage` is already a configurable threshold.
- Mitigation evidence: the current observability slice makes `A-EGS/egs_main.py:get_suspend_info` write `logs/suspend_daily_coverage_<asof>.json` on pass, low-coverage fail, cache-hit, and no-daily skip paths; schema-validated `data_health` v1.2.0 `metrics.suspend_daily_coverage` mirrors the same latest observation. This does not close the watch item because no real weekly run has been reviewed yet.
- Required next action: monitor the first real weekly runs' `logs/suspend_daily_coverage_<asof>.json` and `data_health.metrics.suspend_daily_coverage`; if legitimate market-wide suspension conditions trip the threshold, tune the threshold or add a reviewed calendar / incident override with a focused regression test.

### SR-OPS-002 - Forward tracker writes are non-atomic

- Severity: P1
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: A-short forward evidence / Phase 6b maintenance
- Evidence: `runners/forward_tracker.py:_write_tracker` sorts and writes the tracker with direct `to_csv(TRACKER_CSV, ...)`; an interruption can leave a partial file.
- Accepted calibration: this is an operational integrity issue, not a strategy-alpha finding. It does not affect corrected 5d revalidation if that run does not consume or update the forward tracker.
- Required next action: resolved for the forward-tracker write path. No further action is required for `SR-OPS-002` unless the tracker storage path or writer is redesigned.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set updates `runners/forward_tracker.py:_write_tracker` to write `SCHEMA_COLUMNS` to a same-directory temp CSV, flush and `fsync` the handle, close it, then atomically replace `forward_tracker.csv` with `os.replace`; failures unlink the temp file and leave the existing tracker untouched.
- Verification: `tests.phase6.test_forward_tracker_cache_guard` covers same-directory temp-file naming, one atomic replace call, sorted schema-column output, cleanup of the temp path after success, and preservation of the existing tracker when CSV serialization fails. `python -m unittest discover -s tests\phase6 -v` passed with 25 tests.

### SR-OPS-003 - Direct historical `egs_main.py --as-of` still defaults to live L3 concepts

- Severity: P1
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: A-short historical replay / Phase 6b maintenance
- Evidence: `A-EGS/egs_main.py` argparse still defaults `--l3-mode` to `today`; `SR-EXEC-001` fixed the weekly wrapper but not direct engine invocation.
- Accepted calibration: current `runners/weekly_screening.ps1` protects historical official-output runs, so this is now a direct-engine / ad hoc replay risk. It does not block corrected 5d revalidation if no cohort regeneration occurs.
- Required next action: resolved for direct engine invocation. No further action is required for `SR-OPS-003` unless the L3 CLI contract is redesigned.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set adds `A-EGS/egs_main.py --allow-historical-live-l3` and rejects non-current `--as-of` + `--l3-mode=today` unless that explicit non-evidence / live-concept declaration is present. `runners/backtest_rank.py` now passes the declaration only for smoke-mode historical `today` L3 generation; production/default evidence paths stay on `neutralize` / `pit`.
- Verification: `tests.phase6.test_egs_main_l3_guard` covers historical default rejection, `pit` / `neutralize` acceptance, explicit live-L3 declaration, and current-date `today` acceptance. `tests.test_backtest_rank_phase3` covers the smoke command adding `--allow-historical-live-l3` only when requested.

### SR-DATA-002 - Severe daily-data insufficiency degrades to neutral stats that can pass filters

- Severity: P2
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: A-short data quality / screening maintenance
- Evidence: `A-EGS/egs_main.py:precompute_stock_stats` returns `_neutral_stats_df` when `all_daily` is empty or too small; neutral rows include `has_crash_veto=False`, and `filter_l0` can skip amount filtering when liquidity fields are all NaN / zero.
- Accepted calibration: this is not evidence that normal runs are contaminated; it is a severe-data-insufficiency path that should not emit normal-looking candidate output.
- Required next action: no active `SR-DATA-002` action. Future daily-stat fallback redesigns must preserve a reviewed non-evidence / hard-fail boundary and must not reintroduce normal-looking neutral pass-through stats.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set updates `A-EGS/egs_main.py:precompute_stock_stats` so empty `all_daily`, fewer than `daily_stats_min_rows`, insufficient rows after matching the stock universe, or no valid close rows now raise `RuntimeError` before L0 can treat neutral liquidity / crash-veto defaults as normal evidence. The former get-daily-all log that promised neutral defaults now states that downstream stat computation will abort.
- Verification: `tests.phase6.test_egs_main_daily_stats_guard` covers empty daily payload rejection, tiny payload rejection, no stock-universe match rejection, and a sufficient payload still computing stats.

### SR-EXEC-003 - Execution drawdown misses open-position mark-to-market

- Severity: P1
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: Phase 5 execution backtest / ship-gate readiness
- Evidence: `runners/backtest_execution.py:simulate_execution` initializes `daily_equity` with `market_value = 0`, appends equity points only around realized exit events, and computes `max_drawdown` from realized cash-only equity. Open-position mark-to-market drawdown is not represented.
- Accepted calibration: current ship-gate status remains default-deny / not-evaluable because other required metrics are missing; the risk is future execution or ship-gate evidence overreading a realized-only drawdown.
- Closure: current execution reports publish `metrics.max_drawdown = null`, and `ship_gate_evaluation.metric_results.max_drawdown.value/passed = null` with an explicit mark-to-market not-evaluable reason. `daily_equity.csv` may still contain realized exit-date cash drawdown diagnostics, but those diagnostics are not used as ship-gate drawdown evidence.
- Verification: `tests.execution.test_backtest_execution` covers the time-stop and multi-trade paths with null drawdown evidence until mark-to-market equity exists.
- Required next action: no active SR-EXEC-003 action. If daily mark-to-market equity is later implemented, add reviewed tests before re-enabling numeric execution drawdown evidence.

### SR-EXEC-004 - Execution assumptions report cooldown / circuit breaker controls that are not simulated

- Severity: P1
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: Phase 5 execution backtest / Phase 8 monitoring
- Evidence: `runners/backtest_execution.py:build_execution_assumptions` reports cooldown and portfolio circuit breaker controls as enabled, while the simulation loop does not enforce those controls.
- Accepted calibration: this is primarily an evidence overclaim / report-contract defect. It does not currently authorize full-size use, but it must be fixed before execution evidence is cited as if these controls were tested.
- Closure: current execution reports set `portfolio_circuit_breaker.enabled = false`, `new_entries_blocked = false`, `existing_positions_action = not_implemented`, and `cooldown.enabled = false`. The declared event-code coverage no longer includes `circuit_breaker` or `cooldown_block`, and report limitations state these controls are not simulated or safety evidence.
- Verification: `tests.execution.test_backtest_execution` covers the generated assumptions and limitations for unimplemented circuit breaker / cooldown controls while preserving schema-valid report output.
- Required next action: no active SR-EXEC-004 action. If these controls are later implemented, add reviewed simulator behavior and event-log tests before reporting them as enabled.

### SR-EXEC-005 - Zero-trade execution reports are aggregated as 0.0% monthly returns

- Severity: P1
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: Phase 5 execution aggregation / ship-gate readiness
- Evidence: `runners/aggregate_execution_reports.py:report_total_return_for_aggregation` returns `0.0` for zero-trade reports when total return is absent, causing those months to enter monthly return, t-stat, and Sharpe calculations as flat observations.
- Accepted calibration: this does not currently pass the full ship gate because other metrics remain missing / not-evaluable; it can still inflate sample count or compress variance when execution evidence is later summarized.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set updates `runners/aggregate_execution_reports.py:report_total_return_for_aggregation` so zero-trade reports with null `metrics.total_return` remain missing / not evaluable for return statistics instead of being imputed as `0.0`. The monthly series, `monthly_return_count`, total-return mean, alpha t-stat, and Sharpe now consume only reports with an explicit numeric `total_return`. The aggregate report contract is bumped to `execution_aggregate_report` v1.1.1 and states the corrected zero-trade semantics.
- Verification: `tests.execution.test_aggregate_execution_reports` reverses the old zero-trade invariant: an input month with `trade_count = 0` and null `total_return` remains in input `month_count` but is excluded from `monthly_return_series` / `monthly_return_count` / `total_return_mean`. `tests.schema.test_execution_aggregate_report_schema` validates v1.1.1 and its zero-trade exclusion description.
- Required next action: no active `SR-EXEC-005` action. If a future reviewed cash-return model emits an explicit numeric return for zero-trade reports, add tests before allowing those observations into return statistics.

### SR-EXEC-006 - Execution aggregate can turn smoke / unbound forward-month inputs into full-size permission

- Severity: P1
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: Phase 5 execution aggregation / ship-gate evidence integrity
- Evidence: `runners/aggregate_execution_reports.py:validate_compatible_reports` only checks that reports share the same `capital_context` summary and `mode`; it does not require `mode == production` for ship-gate permission. `--forward-live-months` is a plain CLI integer with no reviewed forward-tracking evidence artifact / ref binding. `build_ship_gate_evaluation` sets `full_size_allowed = status == "pass"`, and current tests include `tests/execution/test_aggregate_execution_reports.py:test_aggregate_with_benchmark_and_forward_months_can_pass_gate`, which can assert `full_size_allowed == true` from two default smoke reports plus a bare `--forward-live-months 12`.
- Accepted calibration: there is no broker or automatic order path, and current burst research is blocked before execution evidence is used. The risk is evidence / manual-sizing overclaim: the core `>= 12 months forward live` ship-gate requirement can be bypassed by an unbound CLI value, and smoke diagnostics can be presented as full-size manual-use permission.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set updates `runners/aggregate_execution_reports.py` so `execution_aggregate_report` v1.1.0 reads a reviewed `--forward-live-evidence-ref` JSON, validates `review_status='reviewed'`, derives / checks `forward_live_months` from that artifact, and keeps bare `--forward-live-months` diagnostic. The original smoke / bare-forward-month bypass is closed: smoke aggregates remain `not_evaluable` for ship-gate permission even if their numeric diagnostics pass, and bare CLI months cannot satisfy the forward-live metric. The later `SR-EXEC-007` closure further keeps `full_size_allowed` false until capacity / concurrency-adjusted returns are evaluable.
- Verification: `tests.execution.test_aggregate_execution_reports` now reverses the old smoke / bare-forward-month pass invariant, covers production reports without reviewed forward evidence staying `not_evaluable`, covers smoke reports with reviewed evidence staying `not_evaluable`, covers reviewed-evidence month matching, and rejects CLI / evidence month mismatches. `tests.schema.test_execution_aggregate_report_schema` validates the v1.1.0+ schema and required `forward_live_evidence_source` field.

### SR-EXEC-007 - Execution simulator serializes overlapping candidates and reuses bucket cash

- Severity: P2
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: Phase 5 execution simulator / capacity and concurrency modeling
- Evidence: `runners/backtest_execution.py:simulate_execution` loops candidates sequentially. For each candidate it enters, subtracts cash, computes the full exit inside the same iteration, and adds cash back before the next candidate is sized. `calculate_shares` therefore sees cash after the prior candidate has already been closed, even when real holding windows would overlap. The code discloses "does not yet model concurrent open positions" in `limitations`, but there is no durable register gate for the resulting capacity / return distortion.
- Accepted calibration: this is distinct from `SR-CAP-001` bucket-ceiling validation. The risk is concurrency and cash-lock modeling: overlapping trades can reuse the same bucket capital serially, overstating capacity-adjusted returns if execution results are later used as ship-gate-like evidence.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set updates `runners/aggregate_execution_reports.py` and `schemas/execution_aggregate_report.schema.json` to v1.1.2. Aggregates still publish serialized execution return diagnostics, but `ship_gate_evaluation.status` remains `not_evaluable` and `full_size_allowed` remains false because capacity / concurrency-adjusted returns are not evaluable while input reports come from the serialized Phase 5 simulator. Production-mode reports with benchmark, reviewed forward-live evidence, and otherwise passing numeric diagnostics no longer unlock full-size permission.
- Verification: `tests.execution.test_aggregate_execution_reports` reverses the prior production + reviewed-forward-evidence pass path: monthly alpha, Sharpe, drawdown, and forward-live diagnostics can pass, but the aggregate stays `not_evaluable`, `full_size_allowed = false`, and limitations state that capacity / concurrency-adjusted returns are not evaluable. `tests.schema.test_execution_aggregate_report_schema` validates v1.1.2 and the updated full-size permission description.
- Required next action: no active `SR-EXEC-007` evidence-overclaim action. If future work wants true capacity-adjusted execution evidence, implement concurrent holdings, cash locks across holding windows, and continuous portfolio equity tests before removing the not-evaluable gate.

### SR-CAP-001 - Capital ceiling is not validated at state load / sizing boundary

- Severity: P1
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: capital policy / execution backtest / coordinator precondition
- Evidence: `runners/backtest_execution.py:calculate_shares` caps by cash, bucket capital, max position percent, and max positions, but there is no hard validation that the loaded `bucket_capital` respects the market / bucket capital ceiling from policy.
- Accepted calibration: this is a missing validation / clamp, not proof that current fixtures always over-allocate. If state already has `bucket_capital <= ceiling_pct * market_capital`, the current calculation may be fine; if state is hand-edited above ceiling, no code rejects it.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set updates `runners/backtest_execution.py` to validate `capital_context.bucket_capital <= capital_context.market_capital * capital_context.bucket_ceiling_pct` when building the capital context and again before empty / simulated execution uses the bucket capital. Above-ceiling cash-state inputs now raise instead of entering share calculation.
- Verification: `tests.execution.test_backtest_execution` covers a hand-edited A-short cash state with `short_bucket_capital = 200000.0` against market capital `350000.0` and ceiling `0.333333`, expecting a `bucket_capital exceeds bucket ceiling` rejection. `tests.schema.test_execution_backtest_report_schema` and `tests.schema.test_capital_context_schemas` still pass.
- Required next action: no active `SR-CAP-001` action. If a later coordinator intentionally supports manual liquidity transfers or ceiling overrides, add a reviewed state transition and tests before allowing above-ceiling bucket capital into sizing.

### SR-OPS-004 - Weekly xlsx overwrite guard checks a different default path than `egs_main.py`

- Severity: P3
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: A-short weekly operation
- Evidence: `runners/weekly_screening.ps1` checks `A-EGS/Result/egs_tier1_<AsOf>.xlsx`, while `A-EGS/egs_main.py` defaults tier1 xlsx output to `A-EGS/egs_tier1_<AsOf>.xlsx` unless `CONF["xlsx_dir"]` is set.
- Accepted calibration: CSV and official result-directory guards still cover the main evidence outputs; this is a low-risk xlsx overwrite guard gap.
- Required next action: no active `SR-OPS-004` action. If `egs_main.py` later moves xlsx output again, update the weekly guard and regression test together.
- Closure evidence: `runners/weekly_screening.ps1` now checks the actual default xlsx path `A-EGS\egs_tier1_<AsOf>.xlsx` in addition to the legacy guarded `A-EGS\Result` xlsx path and existing CSV / official result-directory outputs.
- Verification: `tests.phase6.test_weekly_screening_guardrails` covers historical refusal when the default root-level xlsx output already exists.

### SR-OPS-005 - Forward tracker cache coverage uses calendar-day approximation

- Severity: P2
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: A-short forward evidence / tracker reliability
- Evidence: `runners/forward_tracker.py:_check_cache_coverage` uses calendar-day shifting to approximate the required trading window.
- Accepted calibration: current producers over-pad enough for normal cases, so this is a weaker assertion rather than confirmed live contamination. Long holidays or unusual calendars can still break the assumption.
- Required next action: no active `SR-OPS-005` action. Future tracker cache coverage changes must keep coverage tied to cached trading-date rows, not only metadata calendar dates.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set updates `runners/forward_tracker.py:_check_cache_coverage` to read `stocks.trade_date` from the shared `forward_daily.pkl` payload, require every pending tracker `as_of` to exist in that cached trading-date sequence, and require `base_idx + max_window` to be present before backfill can reuse the cache. It no longer uses `as_of + calendar days` or metadata end-date alone to decide coverage.
- Verification: `tests.phase6.test_forward_tracker_cache_guard` covers close-only benchmark rejection, same-anchor acceptance, insufficient cached trading-date rejection, missing `as_of` rejection, and a sparse long-calendar case that passes because the required cached trading dates exist.

### SR-OPS-006 - Relisted-stock lookback boundary needs revalidation

- Severity: P3
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: A-short screening maintenance
- Evidence: `A-EGS/egs_main.py:get_relisted_stocks` uses `trade_dates[CONF["suspend_lookback"]]` as the cutoff when enough trade dates exist; this may be an off-by-one or short-calendar semantic issue depending on the intended "lookback" definition.
- Accepted calibration: this is a plausible low-risk boundary defect, not confirmed active contamination.
- Required next action: no active `SR-OPS-006` action. Future relisted-stock lookback changes must preserve inclusive trading-date-window semantics and bump cache keys when cached set semantics change.
- Closure evidence: the reviewed change set adds `_lookback_cutoff_trade_date`, using `lookback - 1` as the descending `trade_dates` cutoff index so `suspend_lookback = 5` covers exactly the latest five trading dates (`trade_dates[0..4]`) instead of six. Short histories fall back to the oldest available date. `get_relisted_stocks` now uses `relisted_<as_of>_v2` so old off-by-one cached relisted sets are not reused.
- Verification: `tests.phase6.test_egs_main_relisted_guard` covers the inclusive five-date cutoff, short-history fallback, relisted membership, and v2 cache key. `python -m unittest discover -s tests\phase6 -v` passed 45 tests.

### SR-RANK-001 - Forward-return status can remain `ok` when conversion leaves NaN

- Severity: P3
- Status: resolved
- Owner phase: rank backtest / forward-tracker compatibility
- Evidence: `runners/backtest_rank.py:attach_forward_returns` catches conversion exceptions and later assigns status `"ok"` even when return values can remain NaN.
- Accepted calibration: current rank statistics drop NaN values, so numeric contamination is low; a status-only consumer such as tracker / reporting code could still overread `"ok"`.
- Required next action: no active `SR-RANK-001` action. Future forward-return status changes must keep `"ok"` reserved for rows whose required return fields converted to finite numeric values.
- Closure evidence: `runners/backtest_rank.py:attach_forward_returns` now sets `pending_return_conversion_failed` and skips the `"ok"` status whenever required close-to-close or T+1 open-to-exit-close return conversion fails, produces NaN, or produces a non-finite value. `ret_*d_status = "ok"` is assigned only after both required return paths have converted successfully.
- Verification: `tests.test_backtest_rank_phase3` adds `test_forward_return_conversion_failure_is_not_ok_status`, proving a non-numeric exit close leaves return fields NaN and marks `ret_5d_status = pending_return_conversion_failed` instead of `"ok"`. `tests.phase6.test_forward_tracker_cache_guard` still passes, covering the status-consuming forward-tracker path.
